   class ArithCastOpGenericAdaptorBase {
   public:
     using Properties = ::mlir::EmptyProperties;
   protected:
     ::mlir::DictionaryAttr odsAttrs;
     ::std::optional<::mlir::OperationName> odsOpName;
     Properties properties;
     ::mlir::RegionRange odsRegions;
   public:
     ArithCastOpGenericAdaptorBase(::mlir::DictionaryAttr attrs, const Properties &properties, ::mlir::RegionRange regions = {}) : odsAttrs(attrs), properties(properties), odsRegions(regions) {  if (odsAttrs)
         odsOpName.emplace("emithls.arith.cast", odsAttrs.getContext());
     }
   
     ArithCastOpGenericAdaptorBase(ArithCastOp op);
   
     std::pair<unsigned, unsigned> getODSOperandIndexAndLength(unsigned index, unsigned odsOperandsSize) {
       return {index, 1};
     }
   
     const Properties &getProperties() {
       return properties;
     }
   
     ::mlir::DictionaryAttr getAttributes() {
       return odsAttrs;
     }
   
   };

   template <typename RangeT>
   class ArithCastOpGenericAdaptor : public detail::ArithCastOpGenericAdaptorBase {
     using ValueT = ::llvm::detail::ValueOfRange<RangeT>;
     using Base = detail::ArithCastOpGenericAdaptorBase;
     static constexpr int odsIndex_from = 0;
   public:
     ArithCastOpGenericAdaptor(RangeT values, ::mlir::DictionaryAttr attrs, const Properties &properties, ::mlir::RegionRange regions = {}) : Base(attrs, properties, regions), odsOperands(values) {}
   
     ArithCastOpGenericAdaptor(RangeT values, ::mlir::DictionaryAttr attrs, ::mlir::PropertyRef properties, ::mlir::RegionRange regions = {}) : ArithCastOpGenericAdaptor(values, attrs, (properties ? *properties.as<Properties *>() : Properties{}), regions) {}
   
     ArithCastOpGenericAdaptor(RangeT values, ::mlir::DictionaryAttr attrs = nullptr) : ArithCastOpGenericAdaptor(values, attrs, Properties{}, {}) {}
   
     ArithCastOpGenericAdaptor(RangeT values, const ArithCastOpGenericAdaptorBase &base) : Base(base), odsOperands(values) {}
   
     template <typename LateInst = ArithCastOp, typename = std::enable_if_t<std::is_same_v<LateInst, ArithCastOp>>>
     ArithCastOpGenericAdaptor(RangeT values, LateInst op) : Base(op), odsOperands(values) {}
   
     std::pair<unsigned, unsigned> getODSOperandIndexAndLength(unsigned index) {
       return Base::getODSOperandIndexAndLength(index, odsOperands.size());
     }
   
     RangeT getODSOperands(unsigned index) {
       auto valueRange = getODSOperandIndexAndLength(index);
       return {std::next(odsOperands.begin(), valueRange.first),
                std::next(odsOperands.begin(), valueRange.first + valueRange.second)};
     }
   
     ValueT getFrom() {
       return (*getODSOperands(0).begin());
     }
   
     RangeT getOperands() {
       return odsOperands;
     }
   
   private:
     RangeT odsOperands;
   };

   class ArithCastOp : public ::mlir::Op<ArithCastOp, ::mlir::OpTrait::ZeroRegions, ::mlir::OpTrait::OneResult, ::mlir::OpTrait::OneTypedResult<::mlir::Type>::Impl, ::mlir::OpTrait::ZeroSuccessors, ::mlir::OpTrait::OneOperand, ::mlir::OpTrait::OpInvariants, ::mlir::ConditionallySpeculatable::Trait, ::mlir::OpTrait::AlwaysSpeculatableImplTrait, ::mlir::MemoryEffectOpInterface::Trait, ::mlir::OpTrait::SameOperandsAndResultShape> {
   public:
     using Op::Op;
     using Op::print;
     using Adaptor = ArithCastOpAdaptor;
     template <typename RangeT>
     using GenericAdaptor = ArithCastOpGenericAdaptor<RangeT>;
     using FoldAdaptor = GenericAdaptor<::llvm::ArrayRef<::mlir::Attribute>>;
     static constexpr int odsIndex_from = 0;
     using Properties = FoldAdaptor::Properties;
     static ::llvm::ArrayRef<::llvm::StringRef> getAttributeNames() {
       return {};
     }
   
     static constexpr ::llvm::StringLiteral getOperationName() {
       return ::llvm::StringLiteral("emithls.arith.cast");
     }
   
     std::pair<unsigned, unsigned> getODSOperandIndexAndLength(unsigned index) {
       return {index, 1};
     }
   
     ::mlir::Operation::operand_range getODSOperands(unsigned index) {
       auto valueRange = getODSOperandIndexAndLength(index);
       return {std::next(getOperation()->operand_begin(), valueRange.first),
                std::next(getOperation()->operand_begin(), valueRange.first + valueRange.second)};
     }
   
     ::mlir::TypedValue<::mlir::Type> getFrom() {
       return ::llvm::cast<::mlir::TypedValue<::mlir::Type>>(*getODSOperands(0).begin());
     }
   
     ::mlir::OpOperand &getFromMutable() {
       auto range = getODSOperandIndexAndLength(0);
       return getOperation()->getOpOperand(range.first);
     }
   
     std::pair<unsigned, unsigned> getODSResultIndexAndLength(unsigned index) {
       return {index, 1};
     }
   
     ::mlir::Operation::result_range getODSResults(unsigned index) {
       auto valueRange = getODSResultIndexAndLength(index);
       return {std::next(getOperation()->result_begin(), valueRange.first),
                std::next(getOperation()->result_begin(), valueRange.first + valueRange.second)};
     }
   
     ::mlir::TypedValue<::mlir::Type> getTo() {
       return ::llvm::cast<::mlir::TypedValue<::mlir::Type>>(*getODSResults(0).begin());
     }
   
     static ::llvm::LogicalResult setPropertiesFromAttr(Properties &prop, ::mlir::Attribute attr, ::llvm::function_ref<::mlir::InFlightDiagnostic()> emitError);
     static ::mlir::Attribute getPropertiesAsAttr(::mlir::MLIRContext *ctx, const Properties &prop);

     static ::llvm::LogicalResult verifyInherentAttrs(::mlir::OperationName opName, ::mlir::NamedAttrList &attrs, llvm::function_ref<::mlir::InFlightDiagnostic()> emitError);
     static void build(::mlir::OpBuilder &odsBuilder, ::mlir::OperationState &odsState, ::mlir::Type to, ::mlir::Value from);
     static ArithCastOp create(::mlir::OpBuilder &builder, ::mlir::Location location, ::mlir::Type to, ::mlir::Value from);
     static ArithCastOp create(::mlir::ImplicitLocOpBuilder &builder, ::mlir::Type to, ::mlir::Value from);
     static void build(::mlir::OpBuilder &odsBuilder, ::mlir::OperationState &odsState, ::mlir::TypeRange resultTypes, ::mlir::Value from);
     static ArithCastOp create(::mlir::OpBuilder &builder, ::mlir::Location location, ::mlir::TypeRange resultTypes, ::mlir::Value from);
     static ArithCastOp create(::mlir::ImplicitLocOpBuilder &builder, ::mlir::TypeRange resultTypes, ::mlir::Value from);

   class ArithCmpOpGenericAdaptorBase {
   public:
     struct Properties {
       using predicateTy = ::mlir::emithls::CmpPredicateAttr;
       predicateTy predicate;
   
       auto getPredicate() const {
         auto &propStorage = this->predicate;
         return ::llvm::cast<::mlir::emithls::CmpPredicateAttr>(propStorage);
       }
       void setPredicate(const ::mlir::emithls::CmpPredicateAttr &propValue) {
         this->predicate = propValue;
       }
       bool operator==(const Properties &rhs) const {
         return 
           rhs.predicate == this->predicate &&
           true;
       }
       bool operator!=(const Properties &rhs) const {
         return !(*this == rhs);
       }
     };
   protected:
     ::mlir::DictionaryAttr odsAttrs;
     ::std::optional<::mlir::OperationName> odsOpName;
     Properties properties;
     ::mlir::RegionRange odsRegions;
   public:
     ArithCmpOpGenericAdaptorBase(::mlir::DictionaryAttr attrs, const Properties &properties, ::mlir::RegionRange regions = {}) : odsAttrs(attrs), properties(properties), odsRegions(regions) {  if (odsAttrs)
         odsOpName.emplace("emithls.arith.cmp", odsAttrs.getContext());
     }
   
     ArithCmpOpGenericAdaptorBase(ArithCmpOp op);
   
     std::pair<unsigned, unsigned> getODSOperandIndexAndLength(unsigned index, unsigned odsOperandsSize) {
       return {index, 1};
     }
   
     const Properties &getProperties() {
       return properties;
     }
   
     ::mlir::DictionaryAttr getAttributes() {
       return odsAttrs;
     }
   
     ::mlir::emithls::CmpPredicateAttr getPredicateAttr() {
       auto attr = ::llvm::cast<::mlir::emithls::CmpPredicateAttr>(getProperties().predicate);
       return attr;
     }
   
     ::mlir::emithls::CmpPredicate getPredicate();
   };
   
   } // namespace detail

     static ArithCmpOp create(::mlir::ImplicitLocOpBuilder &builder, ::mlir::TypeRange resultTypes, ::mlir::emithls::CmpPredicate predicate, ::mlir::Value lhs, ::mlir::Value rhs);

     ::llvm::LogicalResult verifyInvariantsImpl();
     ::llvm::LogicalResult verifyInvariants();

     void print(::mlir::OpAsmPrinter &_odsPrinter);
     void getEffects(::llvm::SmallVectorImpl<::mlir::SideEffects::EffectInstance<::mlir::MemoryEffects::Effect>> &effects);
   private:
     ::mlir::StringAttr getAttributeNameForIndex(unsigned index) {
       return getAttributeNameForIndex((*this)->getName(), index);
     }
   
     static ::mlir::StringAttr getAttributeNameForIndex(::mlir::OperationName name, unsigned index) {
       assert(index < 1 && "invalid attribute index");
       assert(name.getStringRef() == getOperationName() && "invalid operation name");
       assert(name.isRegistered() && "Operation isn't registered, missing a "
             "dependent dialect loading?");
       return name.getAttributeNames()[index];
     }
   
   public:
   };

   class ArithDataRangeOpGenericAdaptorBase {
   public:
     using Properties = ::mlir::EmptyProperties;
   protected:
     ::mlir::DictionaryAttr odsAttrs;
     ::std::optional<::mlir::OperationName> odsOpName;
     Properties properties;
     ::mlir::RegionRange odsRegions;
   public:
     ArithDataRangeOpGenericAdaptorBase(::mlir::DictionaryAttr attrs, const Properties &properties, ::mlir::RegionRange regions = {}) : odsAttrs(attrs), properties(properties), odsRegions(regions) {  if (odsAttrs)
         odsOpName.emplace("emithls.arith.data_range", odsAttrs.getContext());
     }
   
     ArithDataRangeOpGenericAdaptorBase(ArithDataRangeOp op);
   
     std::pair<unsigned, unsigned> getODSOperandIndexAndLength(unsigned index, unsigned odsOperandsSize) {
       return {index, 1};
     }
   
     const Properties &getProperties() {
       return properties;
     }
   
     ::mlir::DictionaryAttr getAttributes() {
       return odsAttrs;
     }
   
   };
   
   } // namespace detail
   template <typename RangeT>
   class ArithDataRangeOpGenericAdaptor : public detail::ArithDataRangeOpGenericAdaptorBase {
     using ValueT = ::llvm::detail::ValueOfRange<RangeT>;
     using Base = detail::ArithDataRangeOpGenericAdaptorBase;
     static constexpr int odsIndex_data = 0;
     static constexpr int odsIndex_high_bit = 1;
     static constexpr int odsIndex_low_bit = 2;
   public:
     ArithDataRangeOpGenericAdaptor(RangeT values, ::mlir::DictionaryAttr attrs, const Properties &properties, ::mlir::RegionRange regions = {}) : Base(attrs, properties, regions), odsOperands(values) {}
   
     ArithDataRangeOpGenericAdaptor(RangeT values, ::mlir::DictionaryAttr attrs, ::mlir::PropertyRef properties, ::mlir::RegionRange regions = {}) : ArithDataRangeOpGenericAdaptor(values, attrs, (properties ? *properties.as<Properties *>() : Properties{}), regions) {}
   
     ArithDataRangeOpGenericAdaptor(RangeT values, ::mlir::DictionaryAttr attrs = nullptr) : ArithDataRangeOpGenericAdaptor(values, attrs, Properties{}, {}) {}
   
     ArithDataRangeOpGenericAdaptor(RangeT values, const ArithDataRangeOpGenericAdaptorBase &base) : Base(base), odsOperands(values) {}
   
     template <typename LateInst = ArithDataRangeOp, typename = std::enable_if_t<std::is_same_v<LateInst, ArithDataRangeOp>>>
     ArithDataRangeOpGenericAdaptor(RangeT values, LateInst op) : Base(op), odsOperands(values) {}
   
     std::pair<unsigned, unsigned> getODSOperandIndexAndLength(unsigned index) {
       return Base::getODSOperandIndexAndLength(index, odsOperands.size());
     }
   
     RangeT getODSOperands(unsigned index) {
       auto valueRange = getODSOperandIndexAndLength(index);
       return {std::next(odsOperands.begin(), valueRange.first),
                std::next(odsOperands.begin(), valueRange.first + valueRange.second)};
     }
   
     ValueT getData() {
       return (*getODSOperands(0).begin());
     }
   
     ValueT getHighBit() {
       return (*getODSOperands(1).begin());
     }
   
     ValueT getLowBit() {
       return (*getODSOperands(2).begin());
     }
   
     RangeT getOperands() {
       return odsOperands;
     }
   
   private:
     RangeT odsOperands;
   };
   class ArithDataRangeOpAdaptor : public ArithDataRangeOpGenericAdaptor<::mlir::ValueRange> {
   public:
     using ArithDataRangeOpGenericAdaptor::ArithDataRangeOpGenericAdaptor;
     ArithDataRangeOpAdaptor(ArithDataRangeOp op);
   
     ::llvm::LogicalResult verify(::mlir::Location loc);
   };
   class ArithDataRangeOp : public ::mlir::Op<ArithDataRangeOp, ::mlir::OpTrait::ZeroRegions, ::mlir::OpTrait::OneResult, ::mlir::OpTrait::OneTypedResult<::mlir::Type>::Impl, ::mlir::OpTrait::ZeroSuccessors, ::mlir::OpTrait::NOperands<3>::Impl, ::mlir::OpTrait::OpInvariants, ::mlir::ConditionallySpeculatable::Trait, ::mlir::OpTrait::AlwaysSpeculatableImplTrait, ::mlir::MemoryEffectOpInterface::Trait> {
   public:
     using Op::Op;
     using Op::print;
     using Adaptor = ArithDataRangeOpAdaptor;
     template <typename RangeT>
     using GenericAdaptor = ArithDataRangeOpGenericAdaptor<RangeT>;
     using FoldAdaptor = GenericAdaptor<::llvm::ArrayRef<::mlir::Attribute>>;
     static constexpr int odsIndex_data = 0;
     static constexpr int odsIndex_high_bit = 1;
     static constexpr int odsIndex_low_bit = 2;
     using Properties = FoldAdaptor::Properties;
     static ::llvm::ArrayRef<::llvm::StringRef> getAttributeNames() {
       return {};
     }
   
     static constexpr ::llvm::StringLiteral getOperationName() {
       return ::llvm::StringLiteral("emithls.arith.data_range");
     }
   
     std::pair<unsigned, unsigned> getODSOperandIndexAndLength(unsigned index) {
       return {index, 1};
     }
   
     ::mlir::Operation::operand_range getODSOperands(unsigned index) {
       auto valueRange = getODSOperandIndexAndLength(index);
       return {std::next(getOperation()->operand_begin(), valueRange.first),
                std::next(getOperation()->operand_begin(), valueRange.first + valueRange.second)};
     }
   
     ::mlir::TypedValue<::mlir::Type> getData() {
       return ::llvm::cast<::mlir::TypedValue<::mlir::Type>>(*getODSOperands(0).begin());
     }
   
     ::mlir::TypedValue<::mlir::IndexType> getHighBit() {
       return ::llvm::cast<::mlir::TypedValue<::mlir::IndexType>>(*getODSOperands(1).begin());
     }
   
     ::mlir::TypedValue<::mlir::IndexType> getLowBit() {
       return ::llvm::cast<::mlir::TypedValue<::mlir::IndexType>>(*getODSOperands(2).begin());
     }
   
     ::mlir::OpOperand &getDataMutable() {
       auto range = getODSOperandIndexAndLength(0);
       return getOperation()->getOpOperand(range.first);
     }
   
     ::mlir::OpOperand &getHighBitMutable() {
       auto range = getODSOperandIndexAndLength(1);
       return getOperation()->getOpOperand(range.first);
     }
   
     ::mlir::OpOperand &getLowBitMutable() {
       auto range = getODSOperandIndexAndLength(2);
       return getOperation()->getOpOperand(range.first);
     }
   
     std::pair<unsigned, unsigned> getODSResultIndexAndLength(unsigned index) {
       return {index, 1};
     }
   
     ::mlir::Operation::result_range getODSResults(unsigned index) {
       auto valueRange = getODSResultIndexAndLength(index);
       return {std::next(getOperation()->result_begin(), valueRange.first),
                std::next(getOperation()->result_begin(), valueRange.first + valueRange.second)};
     }
   
     ::mlir::TypedValue<::mlir::Type> getResult() {
       return ::llvm::cast<::mlir::TypedValue<::mlir::Type>>(*getODSResults(0).begin());
     }
   
     static ::llvm::LogicalResult setPropertiesFromAttr(Properties &prop, ::mlir::Attribute attr, ::llvm::function_ref<::mlir::InFlightDiagnostic()> emitError);
     static ::mlir::Attribute getPropertiesAsAttr(::mlir::MLIRContext *ctx, const Properties &prop);
     static llvm::hash_code computePropertiesHash(const Properties &prop);
     static std::optional<mlir::Attribute> getInherentAttr(::mlir::MLIRContext *ctx, const Properties &prop, llvm::StringRef name);
     static void setInherentAttr(Properties &prop, llvm::StringRef name, mlir::Attribute value);
     static void populateInherentAttrs(::mlir::MLIRContext *ctx, const Properties &prop, ::mlir::NamedAttrList &attrs);
     static ::llvm::LogicalResult verifyInherentAttrs(::mlir::OperationName opName, ::mlir::NamedAttrList &attrs, llvm::function_ref<::mlir::InFlightDiagnostic()> emitError);
     static void build(::mlir::OpBuilder &odsBuilder, ::mlir::OperationState &odsState, ::mlir::Type result, ::mlir::Value data, ::mlir::Value high_bit, ::mlir::Value low_bit);
     static ArithDataRangeOp create(::mlir::OpBuilder &builder, ::mlir::Location location, ::mlir::Type result, ::mlir::Value data, ::mlir::Value high_bit, ::mlir::Value low_bit);
     static ArithDataRangeOp create(::mlir::ImplicitLocOpBuilder &builder, ::mlir::Type result, ::mlir::Value data, ::mlir::Value high_bit, ::mlir::Value low_bit);
     static void build(::mlir::OpBuilder &odsBuilder, ::mlir::OperationState &odsState, ::mlir::TypeRange resultTypes, ::mlir::Value data, ::mlir::Value high_bit, ::mlir::Value low_bit);
     static ArithDataRangeOp create(::mlir::OpBuilder &builder, ::mlir::Location location, ::mlir::TypeRange resultTypes, ::mlir::Value data, ::mlir::Value high_bit, ::mlir::Value low_bit);

   class ArithDivOp : public ::mlir::Op<ArithDivOp, ::mlir::OpTrait::ZeroRegions, ::mlir::OpTrait::OneResult, ::mlir::OpTrait::OneTypedResult<::mlir::Type>::Impl, ::mlir::OpTrait::ZeroSuccessors, ::mlir::OpTrait::NOperands<2>::Impl, ::mlir::OpTrait::OpInvariants, ::mlir::ConditionallySpeculatable::Trait, ::mlir::OpTrait::AlwaysSpeculatableImplTrait, ::mlir::MemoryEffectOpInterface::Trait, ::mlir::OpTrait::SameOperandsAndResultType, ::mlir::InferTypeOpInterface::Trait> {

   namespace detail {
   
   class ArithFusedOpGenericAdaptorBase {
   public:
     struct Properties {
       using op_codeTy = ::mlir::emithls::FusedOperatorAttr;
       op_codeTy op_code;
   
       auto getOpCode() const {
         auto &propStorage = this->op_code;
         return ::llvm::cast<::mlir::emithls::FusedOperatorAttr>(propStorage);
       }
       void setOpCode(const ::mlir::emithls::FusedOperatorAttr &propValue) {
         this->op_code = propValue;
       }
       bool operator==(const Properties &rhs) const {
         return 
           rhs.op_code == this->op_code &&
           true;
       }
       bool operator!=(const Properties &rhs) const {
         return !(*this == rhs);
       }
     };
   protected:
     ::mlir::DictionaryAttr odsAttrs;
     ::std::optional<::mlir::OperationName> odsOpName;
     Properties properties;
     ::mlir::RegionRange odsRegions;
   public:
     ArithFusedOpGenericAdaptorBase(::mlir::DictionaryAttr attrs, const Properties &properties, ::mlir::RegionRange regions = {}) : odsAttrs(attrs), properties(properties), odsRegions(regions) {  if (odsAttrs)
         odsOpName.emplace("emithls.arith.fused", odsAttrs.getContext());
     }
   
     ArithFusedOpGenericAdaptorBase(ArithFusedOp op);
   
     std::pair<unsigned, unsigned> getODSOperandIndexAndLength(unsigned index, unsigned odsOperandsSize) {
       return {index, 1};
     }
   
     const Properties &getProperties() {
       return properties;
     }
   
     ::mlir::DictionaryAttr getAttributes() {
       return odsAttrs;
     }
   
     ::mlir::emithls::FusedOperatorAttr getOpCodeAttr() {
       auto attr = ::llvm::cast<::mlir::emithls::FusedOperatorAttr>(getProperties().op_code);
       return attr;
     }
   
     ::mlir::emithls::FusedOperator getOpCode();
   };
   
   } // namespace detail
   template <typename RangeT>
   class ArithFusedOpGenericAdaptor : public detail::ArithFusedOpGenericAdaptorBase {
     using ValueT = ::llvm::detail::ValueOfRange<RangeT>;
     using Base = detail::ArithFusedOpGenericAdaptorBase;
     static constexpr int odsIndex_acc = 0;
     static constexpr int odsIndex_val = 1;
   public:
     ArithFusedOpGenericAdaptor(RangeT values, ::mlir::DictionaryAttr attrs, const Properties &properties, ::mlir::RegionRange regions = {}) : Base(attrs, properties, regions), odsOperands(values) {}
   
     ArithFusedOpGenericAdaptor(RangeT values, ::mlir::DictionaryAttr attrs, ::mlir::PropertyRef properties, ::mlir::RegionRange regions = {}) : ArithFusedOpGenericAdaptor(values, attrs, (properties ? *properties.as<Properties *>() : Properties{}), regions) {}
   
     ArithFusedOpGenericAdaptor(RangeT values, ::mlir::DictionaryAttr attrs = nullptr) : ArithFusedOpGenericAdaptor(values, attrs, Properties{}, {}) {}
   
     ArithFusedOpGenericAdaptor(RangeT values, const ArithFusedOpGenericAdaptorBase &base) : Base(base), odsOperands(values) {}
   
     template <typename LateInst = ArithFusedOp, typename = std::enable_if_t<std::is_same_v<LateInst, ArithFusedOp>>>
     ArithFusedOpGenericAdaptor(RangeT values, LateInst op) : Base(op), odsOperands(values) {}
   
     std::pair<unsigned, unsigned> getODSOperandIndexAndLength(unsigned index) {
       return Base::getODSOperandIndexAndLength(index, odsOperands.size());
     }
   
     RangeT getODSOperands(unsigned index) {
       auto valueRange = getODSOperandIndexAndLength(index);
       return {std::next(odsOperands.begin(), valueRange.first),
                std::next(odsOperands.begin(), valueRange.first + valueRange.second)};
     }
   
     ValueT getAcc() {
       return (*getODSOperands(0).begin());
     }
   
     ValueT getVal() {
       return (*getODSOperands(1).begin());
     }
   
     RangeT getOperands() {
       return odsOperands;
     }
   
   private:
     RangeT odsOperands;
   };

                std::next(getOperation()->operand_begin(), valueRange.first + valueRange.second)};

       return getAttributeNameForIndex((*this)->getName(), index);
     }
   
     static ::mlir::StringAttr getAttributeNameForIndex(::mlir::OperationName name, unsigned index) {
       assert(index < 1 && "invalid attribute index");
       assert(name.getStringRef() == getOperationName() && "invalid operation name");
       assert(name.isRegistered() && "Operation isn't registered, missing a "
             "dependent dialect loading?");
       return name.getAttributeNames()[index];

         return success();

     static void build(::mlir::OpBuilder &odsBuilder, ::mlir::OperationState &odsState, ::mlir::Type result, ::mlir::ValueRange values);
     static ArithLogicalOrOp create(::mlir::OpBuilder &builder, ::mlir::Location location, ::mlir::Type result, ::mlir::ValueRange values);
     static ArithLogicalOrOp create(::mlir::ImplicitLocOpBuilder &builder, ::mlir::Type result, ::mlir::ValueRange values);
     static void build(::mlir::OpBuilder &, ::mlir::OperationState &odsState, ::mlir::TypeRange resultTypes, ::mlir::ValueRange operands, ::llvm::ArrayRef<::mlir::NamedAttribute> attributes = {});

     static ArithLogicalOrOp create(::mlir::OpBuilder &builder, ::mlir::Location location, ::mlir::ValueRange operands, const Properties &properties, ::llvm::ArrayRef<::mlir::NamedAttribute> discardableAttributes = {});

     ::llvm::LogicalResult verifyInvariantsImpl();
     ::llvm::LogicalResult verifyInvariants();
     static ::mlir::ParseResult parse(::mlir::OpAsmParser &parser, ::mlir::OperationState &result);
     void print(::mlir::OpAsmPrinter &_odsPrinter);
     void getEffects(::llvm::SmallVectorImpl<::mlir::SideEffects::EffectInstance<::mlir::MemoryEffects::Effect>> &effects);
   public:
     LogicalResult verify() {
         if (getValues().size() < 2) {
             return emitOpError("expected at least 2 operands");
         }
         return success();
     }
   };

   template <typename RangeT>
   class ArithMulOpGenericAdaptor : public detail::ArithMulOpGenericAdaptorBase {
     using ValueT = ::llvm::detail::ValueOfRange<RangeT>;
     using Base = detail::ArithMulOpGenericAdaptorBase;
     static constexpr int odsIndex_lhs = 0;
     static constexpr int odsIndex_rhs = 1;
   public:
     ArithMulOpGenericAdaptor(RangeT values, ::mlir::DictionaryAttr attrs, const Properties &properties, ::mlir::RegionRange regions = {}) : Base(attrs, properties, regions), odsOperands(values) {}
   
     ArithMulOpGenericAdaptor(RangeT values, ::mlir::DictionaryAttr attrs, ::mlir::PropertyRef properties, ::mlir::RegionRange regions = {}) : ArithMulOpGenericAdaptor(values, attrs, (properties ? *properties.as<Properties *>() : Properties{}), regions) {}
   
     ArithMulOpGenericAdaptor(RangeT values, ::mlir::DictionaryAttr attrs = nullptr) : ArithMulOpGenericAdaptor(values, attrs, Properties{}, {}) {}
   
     ArithMulOpGenericAdaptor(RangeT values, const ArithMulOpGenericAdaptorBase &base) : Base(base), odsOperands(values) {}
   
     template <typename LateInst = ArithMulOp, typename = std::enable_if_t<std::is_same_v<LateInst, ArithMulOp>>>
     ArithMulOpGenericAdaptor(RangeT values, LateInst op) : Base(op), odsOperands(values) {}
   
     std::pair<unsigned, unsigned> getODSOperandIndexAndLength(unsigned index) {
       return Base::getODSOperandIndexAndLength(index, odsOperands.size());
     }
   
     RangeT getODSOperands(unsigned index) {
       auto valueRange = getODSOperandIndexAndLength(index);
       return {std::next(odsOperands.begin(), valueRange.first),
                std::next(odsOperands.begin(), valueRange.first + valueRange.second)};
     }
   
     ValueT getLhs() {
       return (*getODSOperands(0).begin());
     }
   
     ValueT getRhs() {
       return (*getODSOperands(1).begin());
     }
   
     RangeT getOperands() {
       return odsOperands;
     }
   
   private:
     RangeT odsOperands;
   };

         odsOpName.emplace("emithls.arith.rem", odsAttrs.getContext());
     }
   
     ArithRemOpGenericAdaptorBase(ArithRemOp op);
   
     std::pair<unsigned, unsigned> getODSOperandIndexAndLength(unsigned index, unsigned odsOperandsSize) {
       return {index, 1};
     }

       return properties;
     }
   
     ::mlir::DictionaryAttr getAttributes() {
       return odsAttrs;
     }
   
   };

   template <typename RangeT>
   class ArithRemOpGenericAdaptor : public detail::ArithRemOpGenericAdaptorBase {
     using ValueT = ::llvm::detail::ValueOfRange<RangeT>;
     using Base = detail::ArithRemOpGenericAdaptorBase;
     static constexpr int odsIndex_lhs = 0;
     static constexpr int odsIndex_rhs = 1;
   public:
     ArithRemOpGenericAdaptor(RangeT values, ::mlir::DictionaryAttr attrs, const Properties &properties, ::mlir::RegionRange regions = {}) : Base(attrs, properties, regions), odsOperands(values) {}
   
     ArithRemOpGenericAdaptor(RangeT values, ::mlir::DictionaryAttr attrs, ::mlir::PropertyRef properties, ::mlir::RegionRange regions = {}) : ArithRemOpGenericAdaptor(values, attrs, (properties ? *properties.as<Properties *>() : Properties{}), regions) {}
   
     ArithRemOpGenericAdaptor(RangeT values, ::mlir::DictionaryAttr attrs = nullptr) : ArithRemOpGenericAdaptor(values, attrs, Properties{}, {}) {}
   
     ArithRemOpGenericAdaptor(RangeT values, const ArithRemOpGenericAdaptorBase &base) : Base(base), odsOperands(values) {}
   
     template <typename LateInst = ArithRemOp, typename = std::enable_if_t<std::is_same_v<LateInst, ArithRemOp>>>
     ArithRemOpGenericAdaptor(RangeT values, LateInst op) : Base(op), odsOperands(values) {}
   
     std::pair<unsigned, unsigned> getODSOperandIndexAndLength(unsigned index) {
       return Base::getODSOperandIndexAndLength(index, odsOperands.size());
     }
   
     RangeT getODSOperands(unsigned index) {
       auto valueRange = getODSOperandIndexAndLength(index);
       return {std::next(odsOperands.begin(), valueRange.first),
                std::next(odsOperands.begin(), valueRange.first + valueRange.second)};
     }
   
     ValueT getLhs() {
       return (*getODSOperands(0).begin());
     }
   
     ValueT getRhs() {
       return (*getODSOperands(1).begin());
     }
   
     RangeT getOperands() {
       return odsOperands;
     }
   
   private:
     RangeT odsOperands;
   };

   class ArithShlOpGenericAdaptorBase {
   public:
     using Properties = ::mlir::EmptyProperties;
   protected:
     ::mlir::DictionaryAttr odsAttrs;
     ::std::optional<::mlir::OperationName> odsOpName;
     Properties properties;
     ::mlir::RegionRange odsRegions;
   public:
     ArithShlOpGenericAdaptorBase(::mlir::DictionaryAttr attrs, const Properties &properties, ::mlir::RegionRange regions = {}) : odsAttrs(attrs), properties(properties), odsRegions(regions) {  if (odsAttrs)
         odsOpName.emplace("emithls.arith.shl", odsAttrs.getContext());
     }
   
     ArithShlOpGenericAdaptorBase(ArithShlOp op);
   
     std::pair<unsigned, unsigned> getODSOperandIndexAndLength(unsigned index, unsigned odsOperandsSize) {
       return {index, 1};
     }
   
     const Properties &getProperties() {
       return properties;
     }
   
     ::mlir::DictionaryAttr getAttributes() {
       return odsAttrs;
     }
   
   };
   
   } // namespace detail
   template <typename RangeT>
   class ArithShlOpGenericAdaptor : public detail::ArithShlOpGenericAdaptorBase {
     using ValueT = ::llvm::detail::ValueOfRange<RangeT>;
     using Base = detail::ArithShlOpGenericAdaptorBase;
     static constexpr int odsIndex_lhs = 0;
     static constexpr int odsIndex_rhs = 1;
   public:
     ArithShlOpGenericAdaptor(RangeT values, ::mlir::DictionaryAttr attrs, const Properties &properties, ::mlir::RegionRange regions = {}) : Base(attrs, properties, regions), odsOperands(values) {}
   
     ArithShlOpGenericAdaptor(RangeT values, ::mlir::DictionaryAttr attrs, ::mlir::PropertyRef properties, ::mlir::RegionRange regions = {}) : ArithShlOpGenericAdaptor(values, attrs, (properties ? *properties.as<Properties *>() : Properties{}), regions) {}
   
     ArithShlOpGenericAdaptor(RangeT values, ::mlir::DictionaryAttr attrs = nullptr) : ArithShlOpGenericAdaptor(values, attrs, Properties{}, {}) {}
   
     ArithShlOpGenericAdaptor(RangeT values, const ArithShlOpGenericAdaptorBase &base) : Base(base), odsOperands(values) {}
   
     template <typename LateInst = ArithShlOp, typename = std::enable_if_t<std::is_same_v<LateInst, ArithShlOp>>>
     ArithShlOpGenericAdaptor(RangeT values, LateInst op) : Base(op), odsOperands(values) {}
   
     std::pair<unsigned, unsigned> getODSOperandIndexAndLength(unsigned index) {
       return Base::getODSOperandIndexAndLength(index, odsOperands.size());
     }
   
     RangeT getODSOperands(unsigned index) {
       auto valueRange = getODSOperandIndexAndLength(index);
       return {std::next(odsOperands.begin(), valueRange.first),
                std::next(odsOperands.begin(), valueRange.first + valueRange.second)};
     }
   
     ValueT getLhs() {
       return (*getODSOperands(0).begin());
     }
   
     ValueT getRhs() {
       return (*getODSOperands(1).begin());
     }
   
     RangeT getOperands() {
       return odsOperands;
     }
   
   private:
     RangeT odsOperands;
   };
   class ArithShlOpAdaptor : public ArithShlOpGenericAdaptor<::mlir::ValueRange> {
   public:
     using ArithShlOpGenericAdaptor::ArithShlOpGenericAdaptor;
     ArithShlOpAdaptor(ArithShlOp op);
   
     ::llvm::LogicalResult verify(::mlir::Location loc);
   };
   class ArithShlOp : public ::mlir::Op<ArithShlOp, ::mlir::OpTrait::ZeroRegions, ::mlir::OpTrait::OneResult, ::mlir::OpTrait::OneTypedResult<::mlir::Type>::Impl, ::mlir::OpTrait::ZeroSuccessors, ::mlir::OpTrait::NOperands<2>::Impl, ::mlir::OpTrait::OpInvariants, ::mlir::ConditionallySpeculatable::Trait, ::mlir::OpTrait::AlwaysSpeculatableImplTrait, ::mlir::MemoryEffectOpInterface::Trait, ::mlir::OpTrait::SameOperandsAndResultType, ::mlir::InferTypeOpInterface::Trait> {
   public:
     using Op::Op;
     using Op::print;
     using Adaptor = ArithShlOpAdaptor;
     template <typename RangeT>
     using GenericAdaptor = ArithShlOpGenericAdaptor<RangeT>;
     using FoldAdaptor = GenericAdaptor<::llvm::ArrayRef<::mlir::Attribute>>;
     static constexpr int odsIndex_lhs = 0;
     static constexpr int odsIndex_rhs = 1;
     using Properties = FoldAdaptor::Properties;
     static ::llvm::ArrayRef<::llvm::StringRef> getAttributeNames() {
       return {};
     }
   
     static constexpr ::llvm::StringLiteral getOperationName() {
       return ::llvm::StringLiteral("emithls.arith.shl");
     }
   
     std::pair<unsigned, unsigned> getODSOperandIndexAndLength(unsigned index) {
       return {index, 1};
     }
   
     ::mlir::Operation::operand_range getODSOperands(unsigned index) {
       auto valueRange = getODSOperandIndexAndLength(index);
       return {std::next(getOperation()->operand_begin(), valueRange.first),
                std::next(getOperation()->operand_begin(), valueRange.first + valueRange.second)};
     }
   
     ::mlir::TypedValue<::mlir::Type> getLhs() {
       return ::llvm::cast<::mlir::TypedValue<::mlir::Type>>(*getODSOperands(0).begin());
     }
   
     ::mlir::TypedValue<::mlir::Type> getRhs() {
       return ::llvm::cast<::mlir::TypedValue<::mlir::Type>>(*getODSOperands(1).begin());
     }

       auto range = getODSOperandIndexAndLength(0);
       return getOperation()->getOpOperand(range.first);
     }
   
     ::mlir::OpOperand &getRhsMutable() {
       auto range = getODSOperandIndexAndLength(1);
       return getOperation()->getOpOperand(range.first);
     }
   
     std::pair<unsigned, unsigned> getODSResultIndexAndLength(unsigned index) {
       return {index, 1};
     }
   
     ::mlir::Operation::result_range getODSResults(unsigned index) {
       auto valueRange = getODSResultIndexAndLength(index);
       return {std::next(getOperation()->result_begin(), valueRange.first),
                std::next(getOperation()->result_begin(), valueRange.first + valueRange.second)};
     }
   
     ::mlir::TypedValue<::mlir::Type> getResult() {
       return ::llvm::cast<::mlir::TypedValue<::mlir::Type>>(*getODSResults(0).begin());
     }

     static void populateInherentAttrs(::mlir::MLIRContext *ctx, const Properties &prop, ::mlir::NamedAttrList &attrs);
     static ::llvm::LogicalResult verifyInherentAttrs(::mlir::OperationName opName, ::mlir::NamedAttrList &attrs, llvm::function_ref<::mlir::InFlightDiagnostic()> emitError);
     static void build(::mlir::OpBuilder &odsBuilder, ::mlir::OperationState &odsState, ::mlir::Type result, ::mlir::Value lhs, ::mlir::Value rhs);

     }

   template <typename RangeT>
   class ArithSubOpGenericAdaptor : public detail::ArithSubOpGenericAdaptorBase {
     using ValueT = ::llvm::detail::ValueOfRange<RangeT>;
     using Base = detail::ArithSubOpGenericAdaptorBase;
     static constexpr int odsIndex_lhs = 0;
     static constexpr int odsIndex_rhs = 1;
   public:
     ArithSubOpGenericAdaptor(RangeT values, ::mlir::DictionaryAttr attrs, const Properties &properties, ::mlir::RegionRange regions = {}) : Base(attrs, properties, regions), odsOperands(values) {}
   
     ArithSubOpGenericAdaptor(RangeT values, ::mlir::DictionaryAttr attrs, ::mlir::PropertyRef properties, ::mlir::RegionRange regions = {}) : ArithSubOpGenericAdaptor(values, attrs, (properties ? *properties.as<Properties *>() : Properties{}), regions) {}
   
     ArithSubOpGenericAdaptor(RangeT values, ::mlir::DictionaryAttr attrs = nullptr) : ArithSubOpGenericAdaptor(values, attrs, Properties{}, {}) {}
   
     ArithSubOpGenericAdaptor(RangeT values, const ArithSubOpGenericAdaptorBase &base) : Base(base), odsOperands(values) {}
   
     template <typename LateInst = ArithSubOp, typename = std::enable_if_t<std::is_same_v<LateInst, ArithSubOp>>>
     ArithSubOpGenericAdaptor(RangeT values, LateInst op) : Base(op), odsOperands(values) {}
   
     std::pair<unsigned, unsigned> getODSOperandIndexAndLength(unsigned index) {
       return Base::getODSOperandIndexAndLength(index, odsOperands.size());
     }
   
     RangeT getODSOperands(unsigned index) {
       auto valueRange = getODSOperandIndexAndLength(index);
       return {std::next(odsOperands.begin(), valueRange.first),
                std::next(odsOperands.begin(), valueRange.first + valueRange.second)};
     }
   
     ValueT getLhs() {
       return (*getODSOperands(0).begin());
     }
   
     ValueT getRhs() {
       return (*getODSOperands(1).begin());
     }
   
     RangeT getOperands() {
       return odsOperands;
     }
   
   private:
     RangeT odsOperands;
   };

     ArrayPointerWriteOpGenericAdaptorBase(::mlir::DictionaryAttr attrs, const Properties &properties, ::mlir::RegionRange regions = {}) : odsAttrs(attrs), properties(properties), odsRegions(regions) {  if (odsAttrs)
         odsOpName.emplace("emithls.array.ptr_write", odsAttrs.getContext());
     }
   
     ArrayPointerWriteOpGenericAdaptorBase(ArrayPointerWriteOp op);
   
     std::pair<unsigned, unsigned> getODSOperandIndexAndLength(unsigned index, unsigned odsOperandsSize);
     const Properties &getProperties() {
       return properties;
     }
   
     ::mlir::DictionaryAttr getAttributes() {
       return odsAttrs;
     }
   
   };
   
   } // namespace detail
   template <typename RangeT>
   class ArrayPointerWriteOpGenericAdaptor : public detail::ArrayPointerWriteOpGenericAdaptorBase {
     using ValueT = ::llvm::detail::ValueOfRange<RangeT>;
     using Base = detail::ArrayPointerWriteOpGenericAdaptorBase;
     static constexpr int odsIndex_value = 0;
     static constexpr int odsIndex_pointer = 1;
     static constexpr int odsIndex_indices = 2;
   public:
     ArrayPointerWriteOpGenericAdaptor(RangeT values, ::mlir::DictionaryAttr attrs, const Properties &properties, ::mlir::RegionRange regions = {}) : Base(attrs, properties, regions), odsOperands(values) {}
   
     ArrayPointerWriteOpGenericAdaptor(RangeT values, ::mlir::DictionaryAttr attrs, ::mlir::PropertyRef properties, ::mlir::RegionRange regions = {}) : ArrayPointerWriteOpGenericAdaptor(values, attrs, (properties ? *properties.as<Properties *>() : Properties{}), regions) {}
   
     ArrayPointerWriteOpGenericAdaptor(RangeT values, ::mlir::DictionaryAttr attrs = nullptr) : ArrayPointerWriteOpGenericAdaptor(values, attrs, Properties{}, {}) {}
   
     ArrayPointerWriteOpGenericAdaptor(RangeT values, const ArrayPointerWriteOpGenericAdaptorBase &base) : Base(base), odsOperands(values) {}
   
     template <typename LateInst = ArrayPointerWriteOp, typename = std::enable_if_t<std::is_same_v<LateInst, ArrayPointerWriteOp>>>
     ArrayPointerWriteOpGenericAdaptor(RangeT values, LateInst op) : Base(op), odsOperands(values) {}
   
     std::pair<unsigned, unsigned> getODSOperandIndexAndLength(unsigned index) {
       return Base::getODSOperandIndexAndLength(index, odsOperands.size());
     }
   
     RangeT getODSOperands(unsigned index) {
       auto valueRange = getODSOperandIndexAndLength(index);
       return {std::next(odsOperands.begin(), valueRange.first),
                std::next(odsOperands.begin(), valueRange.first + valueRange.second)};
     }
   
     ValueT getValue() {
       return (*getODSOperands(0).begin());
     }
   
     ValueT getPointer() {
       return (*getODSOperands(1).begin());
     }
   
     RangeT getIndices() {
       return getODSOperands(2);
     }
   
     RangeT getOperands() {
       return odsOperands;
     }
   
   private:
     RangeT odsOperands;
   };

   class ArrayPointerWriteOp : public ::mlir::Op<ArrayPointerWriteOp, ::mlir::OpTrait::ZeroRegions, ::mlir::OpTrait::ZeroResults, ::mlir::OpTrait::ZeroSuccessors, ::mlir::OpTrait::AtLeastNOperands<2>::Impl, ::mlir::OpTrait::OpInvariants, ::mlir::MemoryEffectOpInterface::Trait> {
   public:
     using Op::Op;
     using Op::print;
     using Adaptor = ArrayPointerWriteOpAdaptor;
     template <typename RangeT>
     using GenericAdaptor = ArrayPointerWriteOpGenericAdaptor<RangeT>;
     using FoldAdaptor = GenericAdaptor<::llvm::ArrayRef<::mlir::Attribute>>;
     static constexpr int odsIndex_value = 0;
     static constexpr int odsIndex_pointer = 1;
     static constexpr int odsIndex_indices = 2;
     using Properties = FoldAdaptor::Properties;
     static ::llvm::ArrayRef<::llvm::StringRef> getAttributeNames() {
       return {};
     }
   
     static constexpr ::llvm::StringLiteral getOperationName() {
       return ::llvm::StringLiteral("emithls.array.ptr_write");
     }
   
     std::pair<unsigned, unsigned> getODSOperandIndexAndLength(unsigned index);
     ::mlir::Operation::operand_range getODSOperands(unsigned index) {
       auto valueRange = getODSOperandIndexAndLength(index);
       return {std::next(getOperation()->operand_begin(), valueRange.first),
                std::next(getOperation()->operand_begin(), valueRange.first + valueRange.second)};
     }
   
     ::mlir::TypedValue<::mlir::Type> getValue() {
       return ::llvm::cast<::mlir::TypedValue<::mlir::Type>>(*getODSOperands(0).begin());
     }
   
     ::mlir::TypedValue<::mlir::emithls::PointerType> getPointer() {
       return ::llvm::cast<::mlir::TypedValue<::mlir::emithls::PointerType>>(*getODSOperands(1).begin());
     }
   
     ::mlir::Operation::operand_range getIndices() {
       return getODSOperands(2);
     }
   
     ::mlir::OpOperand &getValueMutable() {
       auto range = getODSOperandIndexAndLength(0);
       return getOperation()->getOpOperand(range.first);
     }
   
     ::mlir::OpOperand &getPointerMutable() {
       auto range = getODSOperandIndexAndLength(1);
       return getOperation()->getOpOperand(range.first);
     }
   
     ::mlir::MutableOperandRange getIndicesMutable();
     std::pair<unsigned, unsigned> getODSResultIndexAndLength(unsigned index) {
       return {index, 1};
     }
   
     ::mlir::Operation::result_range getODSResults(unsigned index) {
       auto valueRange = getODSResultIndexAndLength(index);
       return {std::next(getOperation()->result_begin(), valueRange.first),
                std::next(getOperation()->result_begin(), valueRange.first + valueRange.second)};
     }

     static ::mlir::Attribute getPropertiesAsAttr(::mlir::MLIRContext *ctx, const Properties &prop);

     static void setInherentAttr(Properties &prop, llvm::StringRef name, mlir::Attribute value);
     static void populateInherentAttrs(::mlir::MLIRContext *ctx, const Properties &prop, ::mlir::NamedAttrList &attrs);
     static ::llvm::LogicalResult verifyInherentAttrs(::mlir::OperationName opName, ::mlir::NamedAttrList &attrs, llvm::function_ref<::mlir::InFlightDiagnostic()> emitError);
     static void build(::mlir::OpBuilder &odsBuilder, ::mlir::OperationState &odsState, ::mlir::Value value, ::mlir::Value pointer, ::mlir::ValueRange indices);
     static ArrayPointerWriteOp create(::mlir::OpBuilder &builder, ::mlir::Location location, ::mlir::Value value, ::mlir::Value pointer, ::mlir::ValueRange indices);
     static ArrayPointerWriteOp create(::mlir::ImplicitLocOpBuilder &builder, ::mlir::Value value, ::mlir::Value pointer, ::mlir::ValueRange indices);
     static void build(::mlir::OpBuilder &odsBuilder, ::mlir::OperationState &odsState, ::mlir::TypeRange resultTypes, ::mlir::Value value, ::mlir::Value pointer, ::mlir::ValueRange indices);
     static ArrayPointerWriteOp create(::mlir::OpBuilder &builder, ::mlir::Location location, ::mlir::TypeRange resultTypes, ::mlir::Value value, ::mlir::Value pointer, ::mlir::ValueRange indices);
     static ArrayPointerWriteOp create(::mlir::ImplicitLocOpBuilder &builder, ::mlir::TypeRange resultTypes, ::mlir::Value value, ::mlir::Value pointer, ::mlir::ValueRange indices);

     static ArrayPointerWriteOp create(::mlir::ImplicitLocOpBuilder &builder, ::mlir::TypeRange resultTypes, ::mlir::ValueRange operands, const Properties &properties, ::llvm::ArrayRef<::mlir::NamedAttribute> discardableAttributes = {});
     ::llvm::LogicalResult verifyInvariantsImpl();

     void print(::mlir::OpAsmPrinter &_odsPrinter);

                std::next(getOperation()->operand_begin(), valueRange.first + valueRange.second)};
     }
   
     ::mlir::TypedValue<::mlir::Type> getValue() {
       return ::llvm::cast<::mlir::TypedValue<::mlir::Type>>(*getODSOperands(0).begin());
     }
   
     ::mlir::TypedValue<::mlir::emithls::ArrayType> getArray() {
       return ::llvm::cast<::mlir::TypedValue<::mlir::emithls::ArrayType>>(*getODSOperands(1).begin());
     }
   
     ::mlir::Operation::operand_range getIndices() {
       return getODSOperands(2);
     }
   
     ::mlir::OpOperand &getValueMutable() {
       auto range = getODSOperandIndexAndLength(0);
       return getOperation()->getOpOperand(range.first);
     }
   
     ::mlir::OpOperand &getArrayMutable() {
       auto range = getODSOperandIndexAndLength(1);
       return getOperation()->getOpOperand(range.first);
     }
   
     ::mlir::MutableOperandRange getIndicesMutable();
     std::pair<unsigned, unsigned> getODSResultIndexAndLength(unsigned index) {
       return {index, 1};
     }
   
     ::mlir::Operation::result_range getODSResults(unsigned index) {
       auto valueRange = getODSResultIndexAndLength(index);
       return {std::next(getOperation()->result_begin(), valueRange.first),
                std::next(getOperation()->result_begin(), valueRange.first + valueRange.second)};
     }
   
     static ::llvm::LogicalResult setPropertiesFromAttr(Properties &prop, ::mlir::Attribute attr, ::llvm::function_ref<::mlir::InFlightDiagnostic()> emitError);
     static ::mlir::Attribute getPropertiesAsAttr(::mlir::MLIRContext *ctx, const Properties &prop);
     static llvm::hash_code computePropertiesHash(const Properties &prop);
     static std::optional<mlir::Attribute> getInherentAttr(::mlir::MLIRContext *ctx, const Properties &prop, llvm::StringRef name);
     static void setInherentAttr(Properties &prop, llvm::StringRef name, mlir::Attribute value);
     static void populateInherentAttrs(::mlir::MLIRContext *ctx, const Properties &prop, ::mlir::NamedAttrList &attrs);
     static ::llvm::LogicalResult verifyInherentAttrs(::mlir::OperationName opName, ::mlir::NamedAttrList &attrs, llvm::function_ref<::mlir::InFlightDiagnostic()> emitError);
     static void build(::mlir::OpBuilder &odsBuilder, ::mlir::OperationState &odsState, ::mlir::Value value, ::mlir::Value array, ::mlir::ValueRange indices);
     static ArrayWriteOp create(::mlir::OpBuilder &builder, ::mlir::Location location, ::mlir::Value value, ::mlir::Value array, ::mlir::ValueRange indices);
     static ArrayWriteOp create(::mlir::ImplicitLocOpBuilder &builder, ::mlir::Value value, ::mlir::Value array, ::mlir::ValueRange indices);
     static void build(::mlir::OpBuilder &odsBuilder, ::mlir::OperationState &odsState, ::mlir::TypeRange resultTypes, ::mlir::Value value, ::mlir::Value array, ::mlir::ValueRange indices);
     static ArrayWriteOp create(::mlir::OpBuilder &builder, ::mlir::Location location, ::mlir::TypeRange resultTypes, ::mlir::Value value, ::mlir::Value array, ::mlir::ValueRange indices);
     static ArrayWriteOp create(::mlir::ImplicitLocOpBuilder &builder, ::mlir::TypeRange resultTypes, ::mlir::Value value, ::mlir::Value array, ::mlir::ValueRange indices);
     static void build(::mlir::OpBuilder &, ::mlir::OperationState &odsState, ::mlir::TypeRange resultTypes, ::mlir::ValueRange operands, ::llvm::ArrayRef<::mlir::NamedAttribute> attributes = {});
     static ArrayWriteOp create(::mlir::OpBuilder &builder, ::mlir::Location location, ::mlir::TypeRange resultTypes, ::mlir::ValueRange operands, ::llvm::ArrayRef<::mlir::NamedAttribute> attributes = {});
     static ArrayWriteOp create(::mlir::ImplicitLocOpBuilder &builder, ::mlir::TypeRange resultTypes, ::mlir::ValueRange operands, ::llvm::ArrayRef<::mlir::NamedAttribute> attributes = {});
     static void build(::mlir::OpBuilder &, ::mlir::OperationState &odsState, ::mlir::TypeRange resultTypes, ::mlir::ValueRange operands, const Properties &properties, ::llvm::ArrayRef<::mlir::NamedAttribute> discardableAttributes = {});
     static ArrayWriteOp create(::mlir::OpBuilder &builder, ::mlir::Location location, ::mlir::TypeRange resultTypes, ::mlir::ValueRange operands, const Properties &properties, ::llvm::ArrayRef<::mlir::NamedAttribute> discardableAttributes = {});
     static ArrayWriteOp create(::mlir::ImplicitLocOpBuilder &builder, ::mlir::TypeRange resultTypes, ::mlir::ValueRange operands, const Properties &properties, ::llvm::ArrayRef<::mlir::NamedAttribute> discardableAttributes = {});
     ::llvm::LogicalResult verifyInvariantsImpl();
     ::llvm::LogicalResult verifyInvariants();
     ::llvm::LogicalResult verify();
     static ::mlir::ParseResult parse(::mlir::OpAsmParser &parser, ::mlir::OperationState &result);
     void print(::mlir::OpAsmPrinter &_odsPrinter);
   public:
     //===------------------------------------------------------------------===//
     // MemoryEffectsOpInterface Methods
     //===------------------------------------------------------------------===//
     void getEffects(
             SmallVectorImpl<SideEffects::EffectInstance<MemoryEffects::Effect>>
             &effects);
   
     //===------------------------------------------------------------------===//
     // Custom Methods
     //===------------------------------------------------------------------===//
   
     ArrayType getArrayType();
   };
   
   } // namespace mlir::emithls

   //===----------------------------------------------------------------------===//
   // ::mlir::emithls::CallOp declarations

   namespace detail {
   
   class CallOpGenericAdaptorBase {
   public:
     struct Properties {
       using arg_attrsTy = ::mlir::ArrayAttr;
       arg_attrsTy arg_attrs;
   
       auto getArgAttrs() const {
         auto &propStorage = this->arg_attrs;
         return ::llvm::dyn_cast_or_null<::mlir::ArrayAttr>(propStorage);
       }
       void setArgAttrs(const ::mlir::ArrayAttr &propValue) {
         this->arg_attrs = propValue;
       }
       using calleeTy = ::mlir::FlatSymbolRefAttr;
       calleeTy callee;
   
       auto getCallee() const {
         auto &propStorage = this->callee;
         return ::llvm::cast<::mlir::FlatSymbolRefAttr>(propStorage);
       }
       void setCallee(const ::mlir::FlatSymbolRefAttr &propValue) {
         this->callee = propValue;
       }
       using res_attrsTy = ::mlir::ArrayAttr;
       res_attrsTy res_attrs;
   
       auto getResAttrs() const {
         auto &propStorage = this->res_attrs;
         return ::llvm::dyn_cast_or_null<::mlir::ArrayAttr>(propStorage);
       }
       void setResAttrs(const ::mlir::ArrayAttr &propValue) {
         this->res_attrs = propValue;
       }
       bool operator==(const Properties &rhs) const {
         return 
           rhs.arg_attrs == this->arg_attrs &&
           rhs.callee == this->callee &&
           rhs.res_attrs == this->res_attrs &&
           true;
       }
       bool operator!=(const Properties &rhs) const {
         return !(*this == rhs);
       }
     };
   protected:
     ::mlir::DictionaryAttr odsAttrs;
     ::std::optional<::mlir::OperationName> odsOpName;
     Properties properties;
     ::mlir::RegionRange odsRegions;
   public:
     CallOpGenericAdaptorBase(::mlir::DictionaryAttr attrs, const Properties &properties, ::mlir::RegionRange regions = {}) : odsAttrs(attrs), properties(properties), odsRegions(regions) {  if (odsAttrs)
         odsOpName.emplace("emithls.call", odsAttrs.getContext());
     }
   
     CallOpGenericAdaptorBase(CallOp op);
   
     std::pair<unsigned, unsigned> getODSOperandIndexAndLength(unsigned index, unsigned odsOperandsSize);
     const Properties &getProperties() {
       return properties;
     }
   
     ::mlir::DictionaryAttr getAttributes() {
       return odsAttrs;
     }
   
     ::mlir::FlatSymbolRefAttr getCalleeAttr() {
       auto attr = ::llvm::cast<::mlir::FlatSymbolRefAttr>(getProperties().callee);
       return attr;
     }
   
     ::llvm::StringRef getCallee();
     ::mlir::ArrayAttr getArgAttrsAttr() {
       auto attr = ::llvm::dyn_cast_or_null<::mlir::ArrayAttr>(getProperties().arg_attrs);
       return attr;
     }
   
     ::std::optional< ::mlir::ArrayAttr > getArgAttrs();
     ::mlir::ArrayAttr getResAttrsAttr() {
       auto attr = ::llvm::dyn_cast_or_null<::mlir::ArrayAttr>(getProperties().res_attrs);
       return attr;
     }
   
     ::std::optional< ::mlir::ArrayAttr > getResAttrs();
   };
   
   } // namespace detail
   template <typename RangeT>
   class CallOpGenericAdaptor : public detail::CallOpGenericAdaptorBase {
     using ValueT = ::llvm::detail::ValueOfRange<RangeT>;
     using Base = detail::CallOpGenericAdaptorBase;
     static constexpr int odsIndex_operands = 0;
   public:
     CallOpGenericAdaptor(RangeT values, ::mlir::DictionaryAttr attrs, const Properties &properties, ::mlir::RegionRange regions = {}) : Base(attrs, properties, regions), odsOperands(values) {}
   
     CallOpGenericAdaptor(RangeT values, ::mlir::DictionaryAttr attrs, ::mlir::PropertyRef properties, ::mlir::RegionRange regions = {}) : CallOpGenericAdaptor(values, attrs, (properties ? *properties.as<Properties *>() : Properties{}), regions) {}
   
     CallOpGenericAdaptor(RangeT values, ::mlir::DictionaryAttr attrs = nullptr) : CallOpGenericAdaptor(values, attrs, Properties{}, {}) {}
   
     CallOpGenericAdaptor(RangeT values, const CallOpGenericAdaptorBase &base) : Base(base), odsOperands(values) {}
   
     template <typename LateInst = CallOp, typename = std::enable_if_t<std::is_same_v<LateInst, CallOp>>>
     CallOpGenericAdaptor(RangeT values, LateInst op) : Base(op), odsOperands(values) {}
   
     std::pair<unsigned, unsigned> getODSOperandIndexAndLength(unsigned index) {
       return Base::getODSOperandIndexAndLength(index, odsOperands.size());
     }
   
     RangeT getODSOperands(unsigned index) {
       auto valueRange = getODSOperandIndexAndLength(index);
       return {std::next(odsOperands.begin(), valueRange.first),
                std::next(odsOperands.begin(), valueRange.first + valueRange.second)};
     }
   
     RangeT getOperands() {
       return getODSOperands(0);
     }
   
   private:
     RangeT odsOperands;
   };
   class CallOpAdaptor : public CallOpGenericAdaptor<::mlir::ValueRange> {
   public:
     using CallOpGenericAdaptor::CallOpGenericAdaptor;
     CallOpAdaptor(CallOp op);
   
     ::llvm::LogicalResult verify(::mlir::Location loc);
   };
   class CallOp : public ::mlir::Op<CallOp, ::mlir::OpTrait::ZeroRegions, ::mlir::OpTrait::VariadicResults, ::mlir::OpTrait::ZeroSuccessors, ::mlir::OpTrait::VariadicOperands, ::mlir::OpTrait::OpInvariants, ::mlir::BytecodeOpInterface::Trait, ::mlir::ArgAndResultAttrsOpInterface::Trait, ::mlir::CallOpInterface::Trait> {
   public:
     using Op::Op;
     using Op::print;
     using Adaptor = CallOpAdaptor;
     template <typename RangeT>
     using GenericAdaptor = CallOpGenericAdaptor<RangeT>;
     using FoldAdaptor = GenericAdaptor<::llvm::ArrayRef<::mlir::Attribute>>;
     static constexpr int odsIndex_operands = 0;
     using Properties = FoldAdaptor::Properties;
     static ::llvm::ArrayRef<::llvm::StringRef> getAttributeNames() {
       static ::llvm::StringRef attrNames[] = {::llvm::StringRef("arg_attrs"), ::llvm::StringRef("callee"), ::llvm::StringRef("res_attrs")};
       return ::llvm::ArrayRef(attrNames);
     }
   
     ::mlir::StringAttr getArgAttrsAttrName() {
       return getAttributeNameForIndex(0);
     }
   
     static ::mlir::StringAttr getArgAttrsAttrName(::mlir::OperationName name) {
       return getAttributeNameForIndex(name, 0);
     }
   
     ::mlir::StringAttr getCalleeAttrName() {
       return getAttributeNameForIndex(1);
     }
   
     static ::mlir::StringAttr getCalleeAttrName(::mlir::OperationName name) {
       return getAttributeNameForIndex(name, 1);
     }
   
     ::mlir::StringAttr getResAttrsAttrName() {
       return getAttributeNameForIndex(2);
     }
   
     static ::mlir::StringAttr getResAttrsAttrName(::mlir::OperationName name) {
       return getAttributeNameForIndex(name, 2);
     }
   
     static constexpr ::llvm::StringLiteral getOperationName() {
       return ::llvm::StringLiteral("emithls.call");
     }
   
     std::pair<unsigned, unsigned> getODSOperandIndexAndLength(unsigned index);
     ::mlir::Operation::operand_range getODSOperands(unsigned index) {
       auto valueRange = getODSOperandIndexAndLength(index);
       return {std::next(getOperation()->operand_begin(), valueRange.first),
                std::next(getOperation()->operand_begin(), valueRange.first + valueRange.second)};
     }
   
     ::mlir::Operation::operand_range getOperands() {
       return getODSOperands(0);
     }
   
     ::mlir::MutableOperandRange getOperandsMutable();
     std::pair<unsigned, unsigned> getODSResultIndexAndLength(unsigned index);
     ::mlir::Operation::result_range getODSResults(unsigned index) {
       auto valueRange = getODSResultIndexAndLength(index);
       return {std::next(getOperation()->result_begin(), valueRange.first),
                std::next(getOperation()->result_begin(), valueRange.first + valueRange.second)};
     }
   
     static ::llvm::LogicalResult setPropertiesFromAttr(Properties &prop, ::mlir::Attribute attr, ::llvm::function_ref<::mlir::InFlightDiagnostic()> emitError);
     static ::mlir::Attribute getPropertiesAsAttr(::mlir::MLIRContext *ctx, const Properties &prop);
     static llvm::hash_code computePropertiesHash(const Properties &prop);
     static std::optional<mlir::Attribute> getInherentAttr(::mlir::MLIRContext *ctx, const Properties &prop, llvm::StringRef name);
     static void setInherentAttr(Properties &prop, llvm::StringRef name, mlir::Attribute value);
     static void populateInherentAttrs(::mlir::MLIRContext *ctx, const Properties &prop, ::mlir::NamedAttrList &attrs);
     static ::llvm::LogicalResult verifyInherentAttrs(::mlir::OperationName opName, ::mlir::NamedAttrList &attrs, llvm::function_ref<::mlir::InFlightDiagnostic()> emitError);
     static ::llvm::LogicalResult readProperties(::mlir::DialectBytecodeReader &reader, ::mlir::OperationState &state);
     void writeProperties(::mlir::DialectBytecodeWriter &writer);
     ::mlir::FlatSymbolRefAttr getCalleeAttr() {
       return ::llvm::cast<::mlir::FlatSymbolRefAttr>(getProperties().callee);
     }
   
     ::llvm::StringRef getCallee();
     ::mlir::ArrayAttr getArgAttrsAttr() {
       return ::llvm::dyn_cast_or_null<::mlir::ArrayAttr>(getProperties().arg_attrs);
     }
   
     ::std::optional< ::mlir::ArrayAttr > getArgAttrs();
     ::mlir::ArrayAttr getResAttrsAttr() {
       return ::llvm::dyn_cast_or_null<::mlir::ArrayAttr>(getProperties().res_attrs);
     }
   
     ::std::optional< ::mlir::ArrayAttr > getResAttrs();
     void setCalleeAttr(::mlir::FlatSymbolRefAttr attr) {
       getProperties().callee = attr;
     }
   
     void setCallee(::llvm::StringRef attrValue);
     void setArgAttrsAttr(::mlir::ArrayAttr attr) {
       getProperties().arg_attrs = attr;
     }
   
     void setResAttrsAttr(::mlir::ArrayAttr attr) {
       getProperties().res_attrs = attr;
     }
   
     ::mlir::Attribute removeArgAttrsAttr() {
         auto attr = getProperties().arg_attrs;
         getProperties().arg_attrs = {};
         return attr;
     }
   
     ::mlir::Attribute removeResAttrsAttr() {
         auto attr = getProperties().res_attrs;
         getProperties().res_attrs = {};
         return attr;
     }
   
     static void build(::mlir::OpBuilder &odsBuilder, ::mlir::OperationState &odsState, FuncOp callee, ValueRange operands = {});
     static CallOp create(::mlir::OpBuilder &builder, ::mlir::Location location, FuncOp callee, ValueRange operands = {});
     static CallOp create(::mlir::ImplicitLocOpBuilder &builder, FuncOp callee, ValueRange operands = {});
     ::llvm::LogicalResult verifyInvariantsImpl();
     ::llvm::LogicalResult verifyInvariants();
     static ::mlir::ParseResult parse(::mlir::OpAsmParser &parser, ::mlir::OperationState &result);
     void print(::mlir::OpAsmPrinter &_odsPrinter);
   private:
     ::mlir::StringAttr getAttributeNameForIndex(unsigned index) {
       return getAttributeNameForIndex((*this)->getName(), index);
     }
   
     static ::mlir::StringAttr getAttributeNameForIndex(::mlir::OperationName name, unsigned index) {
       assert(index < 3 && "invalid attribute index");
       assert(name.getStringRef() == getOperationName() && "invalid operation name");
       assert(name.isRegistered() && "Operation isn't registered, missing a "
             "dependent dialect loading?");
       return name.getAttributeNames()[index];
     }
   
   public:
     //===------------------------------------------------------------------===//
     // CallOpInterface Methods
     //===------------------------------------------------------------------===//
   
     operand_range getArgOperands();
     MutableOperandRange getArgOperandsMutable();
     operand_iterator arg_operand_begin();
     operand_iterator arg_operand_end();
     CallInterfaceCallable getCallableForCallee();
     void setCalleeFromCallable(CallInterfaceCallable callee);
   };
   
   } // namespace mlir::emithls

       return odsRegions;
     }
   
   };

   class ExpressionOp : public ::mlir::Op<ExpressionOp, ::mlir::OpTrait::OneRegion, ::mlir::OpTrait::OneResult, ::mlir::OpTrait::OneTypedResult<::mlir::Type>::Impl, ::mlir::OpTrait::ZeroSuccessors, ::mlir::OpTrait::ZeroOperands, ::mlir::OpTrait::NoRegionArguments, ::mlir::OpTrait::SingleBlock, ::mlir::OpTrait::SingleBlockImplicitTerminator<YieldOp>::Impl, ::mlir::OpTrait::OpInvariants, ::mlir::OpAsmOpInterface::Trait> {
   public:
     using Op::Op;
     using Op::print;
     using Adaptor = ExpressionOpAdaptor;
     template <typename RangeT>
     using GenericAdaptor = ExpressionOpGenericAdaptor<RangeT>;
     using FoldAdaptor = GenericAdaptor<::llvm::ArrayRef<::mlir::Attribute>>;
     using Properties = FoldAdaptor::Properties;
     using ::mlir::OpAsmOpInterface::Trait<ExpressionOp>::getAsmBlockArgumentNames;
     using ::mlir::OpAsmOpInterface::Trait<ExpressionOp>::getAsmBlockNames;
     using ::mlir::OpAsmOpInterface::Trait<ExpressionOp>::getDefaultDialect;
     static ::llvm::ArrayRef<::llvm::StringRef> getAttributeNames() {
       return {};
     }
   
     static constexpr ::llvm::StringLiteral getOperationName() {
       return ::llvm::StringLiteral("emithls.expr");
     }
   
     std::pair<unsigned, unsigned> getODSOperandIndexAndLength(unsigned index) {
       return {index, 1};
     }
   
     ::mlir::Operation::operand_range getODSOperands(unsigned index) {
       auto valueRange = getODSOperandIndexAndLength(index);
       return {std::next(getOperation()->operand_begin(), valueRange.first),
                std::next(getOperation()->operand_begin(), valueRange.first + valueRange.second)};
     }
   
     std::pair<unsigned, unsigned> getODSResultIndexAndLength(unsigned index) {
       return {index, 1};
     }
   
     ::mlir::Operation::result_range getODSResults(unsigned index) {
       auto valueRange = getODSResultIndexAndLength(index);
       return {std::next(getOperation()->result_begin(), valueRange.first),
                std::next(getOperation()->result_begin(), valueRange.first + valueRange.second)};
     }
   
     ::mlir::TypedValue<::mlir::Type> getResult() {
       return ::llvm::cast<::mlir::TypedValue<::mlir::Type>>(*getODSResults(0).begin());
     }
   
     ::mlir::Region &getBody() {
       return (*this)->getRegion(0);
     }
   
     static ::llvm::LogicalResult setPropertiesFromAttr(Properties &prop, ::mlir::Attribute attr, ::llvm::function_ref<::mlir::InFlightDiagnostic()> emitError);
     static ::mlir::Attribute getPropertiesAsAttr(::mlir::MLIRContext *ctx, const Properties &prop);
     static llvm::hash_code computePropertiesHash(const Properties &prop);
     static std::optional<mlir::Attribute> getInherentAttr(::mlir::MLIRContext *ctx, const Properties &prop, llvm::StringRef name);
     static void setInherentAttr(Properties &prop, llvm::StringRef name, mlir::Attribute value);
     static void populateInherentAttrs(::mlir::MLIRContext *ctx, const Properties &prop, ::mlir::NamedAttrList &attrs);
     static ::llvm::LogicalResult verifyInherentAttrs(::mlir::OperationName opName, ::mlir::NamedAttrList &attrs, llvm::function_ref<::mlir::InFlightDiagnostic()> emitError);
     static void build(::mlir::OpBuilder &odsBuilder, ::mlir::OperationState &odsState, Type resultType, function_ref<void(OpBuilder &, Location)> bodyBuilder);
     static ExpressionOp create(::mlir::OpBuilder &builder, ::mlir::Location location, Type resultType, function_ref<void(OpBuilder &, Location)> bodyBuilder);
     static ExpressionOp create(::mlir::ImplicitLocOpBuilder &builder, Type resultType, function_ref<void(OpBuilder &, Location)> bodyBuilder);
     ::llvm::LogicalResult verifyInvariantsImpl();
     ::llvm::LogicalResult verifyInvariants();
     ::llvm::LogicalResult verify();
     static void getCanonicalizationPatterns(::mlir::RewritePatternSet &results, ::mlir::MLIRContext *context);
     void getAsmResultNames(::mlir::OpAsmSetValueNameFn setNameFn);
     static ::mlir::ParseResult parse(::mlir::OpAsmParser &parser, ::mlir::OperationState &result);
     void print(::mlir::OpAsmPrinter &_odsPrinter);
   public:
     Operation* getRootOp();
   };

   //===----------------------------------------------------------------------===//
   // ::mlir::emithls::ForOp declarations
   //===----------------------------------------------------------------------===//
   
   namespace detail {
   
   class ForOpGenericAdaptorBase {
   public:
     struct Properties {
       using lowerBoundTy = ::mlir::IntegerAttr;
       lowerBoundTy lowerBound;
   
       auto getLowerBound() const {
         auto &propStorage = this->lowerBound;
         return ::llvm::cast<::mlir::IntegerAttr>(propStorage);
       }
       void setLowerBound(const ::mlir::IntegerAttr &propValue) {
         this->lowerBound = propValue;
       }
       using stepTy = ::mlir::IntegerAttr;
       stepTy step;
   
       auto getStep() const {
         auto &propStorage = this->step;
         return ::llvm::cast<::mlir::IntegerAttr>(propStorage);
       }
       void setStep(const ::mlir::IntegerAttr &propValue) {
         this->step = propValue;
       }
       using upperBoundTy = ::mlir::IntegerAttr;
       upperBoundTy upperBound;
   
       auto getUpperBound() const {
         auto &propStorage = this->upperBound;
         return ::llvm::cast<::mlir::IntegerAttr>(propStorage);
       }
       void setUpperBound(const ::mlir::IntegerAttr &propValue) {
         this->upperBound = propValue;
       }
       bool operator==(const Properties &rhs) const {
         return 
           rhs.lowerBound == this->lowerBound &&
           rhs.step == this->step &&
           rhs.upperBound == this->upperBound &&
           true;
       }
       bool operator!=(const Properties &rhs) const {
         return !(*this == rhs);
       }
     };
   protected:
     ::mlir::DictionaryAttr odsAttrs;
     ::std::optional<::mlir::OperationName> odsOpName;
     Properties properties;
     ::mlir::RegionRange odsRegions;
   public:
     ForOpGenericAdaptorBase(::mlir::DictionaryAttr attrs, const Properties &properties, ::mlir::RegionRange regions = {}) : odsAttrs(attrs), properties(properties), odsRegions(regions) {  if (odsAttrs)
         odsOpName.emplace("emithls.for", odsAttrs.getContext());
     }
   
     ForOpGenericAdaptorBase(ForOp op);
   
     std::pair<unsigned, unsigned> getODSOperandIndexAndLength(unsigned index, unsigned odsOperandsSize) {
       return {index, 1};
     }
   
     const Properties &getProperties() {
       return properties;
     }
   
     ::mlir::DictionaryAttr getAttributes() {
       return odsAttrs;
     }
   
     ::mlir::IntegerAttr getLowerBoundAttr() {
       auto attr = ::llvm::cast<::mlir::IntegerAttr>(getProperties().lowerBound);
       return attr;
     }
   
     ::llvm::APInt getLowerBound();
     ::mlir::IntegerAttr getUpperBoundAttr() {
       auto attr = ::llvm::cast<::mlir::IntegerAttr>(getProperties().upperBound);
       return attr;
     }
   
     ::llvm::APInt getUpperBound();
     ::mlir::IntegerAttr getStepAttr() {
       auto attr = ::llvm::cast<::mlir::IntegerAttr>(getProperties().step);
       return attr;
     }
   
     ::llvm::APInt getStep();
     ::mlir::Region &getBody() {
       return *odsRegions[0];
     }
   
     ::mlir::RegionRange getRegions() {
       return odsRegions;
     }
   
   };
   
   } // namespace detail
   template <typename RangeT>
   class ForOpGenericAdaptor : public detail::ForOpGenericAdaptorBase {
     using ValueT = ::llvm::detail::ValueOfRange<RangeT>;
     using Base = detail::ForOpGenericAdaptorBase;
   public:
     ForOpGenericAdaptor(RangeT values, ::mlir::DictionaryAttr attrs, const Properties &properties, ::mlir::RegionRange regions = {}) : Base(attrs, properties, regions), odsOperands(values) {}
   
     ForOpGenericAdaptor(RangeT values, ::mlir::DictionaryAttr attrs, ::mlir::PropertyRef properties, ::mlir::RegionRange regions = {}) : ForOpGenericAdaptor(values, attrs, (properties ? *properties.as<Properties *>() : Properties{}), regions) {}
   
     ForOpGenericAdaptor(RangeT values, ::mlir::DictionaryAttr attrs = nullptr) : ForOpGenericAdaptor(values, attrs, Properties{}, {}) {}
   
     ForOpGenericAdaptor(RangeT values, const ForOpGenericAdaptorBase &base) : Base(base), odsOperands(values) {}
   
     template <typename LateInst = ForOp, typename = std::enable_if_t<std::is_same_v<LateInst, ForOp>>>
     ForOpGenericAdaptor(RangeT values, LateInst op) : Base(op), odsOperands(values) {}
   
     std::pair<unsigned, unsigned> getODSOperandIndexAndLength(unsigned index) {
       return Base::getODSOperandIndexAndLength(index, odsOperands.size());
     }
   
     RangeT getODSOperands(unsigned index) {
       auto valueRange = getODSOperandIndexAndLength(index);
       return {std::next(odsOperands.begin(), valueRange.first),
                std::next(odsOperands.begin(), valueRange.first + valueRange.second)};
     }
   
     RangeT getOperands() {
       return odsOperands;
     }
   
   private:
     RangeT odsOperands;
   };
   class ForOpAdaptor : public ForOpGenericAdaptor<::mlir::ValueRange> {
   public:
     using ForOpGenericAdaptor::ForOpGenericAdaptor;
     ForOpAdaptor(ForOp op);
   
     ::llvm::LogicalResult verify(::mlir::Location loc);
   };
   class ForOp : public ::mlir::Op<ForOp, ::mlir::OpTrait::OneRegion, ::mlir::OpTrait::ZeroResults, ::mlir::OpTrait::ZeroSuccessors, ::mlir::OpTrait::ZeroOperands, ::mlir::OpTrait::NoTerminator, ::mlir::OpTrait::OpInvariants, ::mlir::BytecodeOpInterface::Trait, ::mlir::OpTrait::HasRecursiveMemoryEffects, ::mlir::OpAsmOpInterface::Trait> {
   public:
     using Op::Op;
     using Op::print;
     using Adaptor = ForOpAdaptor;
     template <typename RangeT>
     using GenericAdaptor = ForOpGenericAdaptor<RangeT>;
     using FoldAdaptor = GenericAdaptor<::llvm::ArrayRef<::mlir::Attribute>>;
     using Properties = FoldAdaptor::Properties;
     using ::mlir::OpAsmOpInterface::Trait<ForOp>::getAsmResultNames;
     using ::mlir::OpAsmOpInterface::Trait<ForOp>::getAsmBlockNames;
     using ::mlir::OpAsmOpInterface::Trait<ForOp>::getDefaultDialect;
     static ::llvm::ArrayRef<::llvm::StringRef> getAttributeNames() {
       static ::llvm::StringRef attrNames[] = {::llvm::StringRef("lowerBound"), ::llvm::StringRef("step"), ::llvm::StringRef("upperBound")};
       return ::llvm::ArrayRef(attrNames);
     }
   
     ::mlir::StringAttr getLowerBoundAttrName() {
       return getAttributeNameForIndex(0);
     }
   
     static ::mlir::StringAttr getLowerBoundAttrName(::mlir::OperationName name) {
       return getAttributeNameForIndex(name, 0);
     }
   
     ::mlir::StringAttr getStepAttrName() {
       return getAttributeNameForIndex(1);
     }
   
     static ::mlir::StringAttr getStepAttrName(::mlir::OperationName name) {
       return getAttributeNameForIndex(name, 1);
     }
   
     ::mlir::StringAttr getUpperBoundAttrName() {
       return getAttributeNameForIndex(2);
     }
   
     static ::mlir::StringAttr getUpperBoundAttrName(::mlir::OperationName name) {
       return getAttributeNameForIndex(name, 2);
     }
   
     static constexpr ::llvm::StringLiteral getOperationName() {
       return ::llvm::StringLiteral("emithls.for");
     }
   
     std::pair<unsigned, unsigned> getODSOperandIndexAndLength(unsigned index) {
       return {index, 1};
     }
   
     ::mlir::Operation::operand_range getODSOperands(unsigned index) {
       auto valueRange = getODSOperandIndexAndLength(index);
       return {std::next(getOperation()->operand_begin(), valueRange.first),
                std::next(getOperation()->operand_begin(), valueRange.first + valueRange.second)};
     }
   
     std::pair<unsigned, unsigned> getODSResultIndexAndLength(unsigned index) {
       return {index, 1};
     }
   
     ::mlir::Operation::result_range getODSResults(unsigned index) {
       auto valueRange = getODSResultIndexAndLength(index);
       return {std::next(getOperation()->result_begin(), valueRange.first),
                std::next(getOperation()->result_begin(), valueRange.first + valueRange.second)};
     }
   
     ::mlir::Region &getBody() {
       return (*this)->getRegion(0);
     }
   
     static ::llvm::LogicalResult setPropertiesFromAttr(Properties &prop, ::mlir::Attribute attr, ::llvm::function_ref<::mlir::InFlightDiagnostic()> emitError);
     static ::mlir::Attribute getPropertiesAsAttr(::mlir::MLIRContext *ctx, const Properties &prop);
     static llvm::hash_code computePropertiesHash(const Properties &prop);
     static std::optional<mlir::Attribute> getInherentAttr(::mlir::MLIRContext *ctx, const Properties &prop, llvm::StringRef name);
     static void setInherentAttr(Properties &prop, llvm::StringRef name, mlir::Attribute value);
     static void populateInherentAttrs(::mlir::MLIRContext *ctx, const Properties &prop, ::mlir::NamedAttrList &attrs);
     static ::llvm::LogicalResult verifyInherentAttrs(::mlir::OperationName opName, ::mlir::NamedAttrList &attrs, llvm::function_ref<::mlir::InFlightDiagnostic()> emitError);
     static ::llvm::LogicalResult readProperties(::mlir::DialectBytecodeReader &reader, ::mlir::OperationState &state);
     void writeProperties(::mlir::DialectBytecodeWriter &writer);
     ::mlir::IntegerAttr getLowerBoundAttr() {
       return ::llvm::cast<::mlir::IntegerAttr>(getProperties().lowerBound);
     }
   
     ::llvm::APInt getLowerBound();
     ::mlir::IntegerAttr getUpperBoundAttr() {
       return ::llvm::cast<::mlir::IntegerAttr>(getProperties().upperBound);
     }
   
     ::llvm::APInt getUpperBound();
     ::mlir::IntegerAttr getStepAttr() {
       return ::llvm::cast<::mlir::IntegerAttr>(getProperties().step);
     }
   
     ::llvm::APInt getStep();
     void setLowerBoundAttr(::mlir::IntegerAttr attr) {
       getProperties().lowerBound = attr;
     }
   
     void setLowerBound(::llvm::APInt attrValue);
     void setUpperBoundAttr(::mlir::IntegerAttr attr) {
       getProperties().upperBound = attr;
     }
   
     void setUpperBound(::llvm::APInt attrValue);
     void setStepAttr(::mlir::IntegerAttr attr) {
       getProperties().step = attr;
     }
   
     void setStep(::llvm::APInt attrValue);
     static void build(::mlir::OpBuilder &odsBuilder, ::mlir::OperationState &odsState, int64_t lowerBound, int64_t upperBound, int64_t step = 1, function_ref<void(OpBuilder &, Location, ValueRange)> bodyBuilder = nullptr);
     static ForOp create(::mlir::OpBuilder &builder, ::mlir::Location location, int64_t lowerBound, int64_t upperBound, int64_t step = 1, function_ref<void(OpBuilder &, Location, ValueRange)> bodyBuilder = nullptr);
     static ForOp create(::mlir::ImplicitLocOpBuilder &builder, int64_t lowerBound, int64_t upperBound, int64_t step = 1, function_ref<void(OpBuilder &, Location, ValueRange)> bodyBuilder = nullptr);
     static void build(::mlir::OpBuilder &odsBuilder, ::mlir::OperationState &odsState, Attribute lowerBound, Attribute upperBound, Attribute step, function_ref<void(OpBuilder &, Location, ValueRange)> bodyBuilder = nullptr);
     static ForOp create(::mlir::OpBuilder &builder, ::mlir::Location location, Attribute lowerBound, Attribute upperBound, Attribute step, function_ref<void(OpBuilder &, Location, ValueRange)> bodyBuilder = nullptr);
     static ForOp create(::mlir::ImplicitLocOpBuilder &builder, Attribute lowerBound, Attribute upperBound, Attribute step, function_ref<void(OpBuilder &, Location, ValueRange)> bodyBuilder = nullptr);
     static ::mlir::ParseResult parse(::mlir::OpAsmParser &parser, ::mlir::OperationState &result);
     void print(::mlir::OpAsmPrinter &p);
     ::llvm::LogicalResult verifyInvariantsImpl();
     ::llvm::LogicalResult verifyInvariants();
     ::llvm::LogicalResult verify();
     static void getCanonicalizationPatterns(::mlir::RewritePatternSet &results, ::mlir::MLIRContext *context);
     void getAsmBlockArgumentNames(::mlir::Region&region, ::mlir::OpAsmSetValueNameFn setNameFn);
     static ::llvm::LogicalResult setPropertiesFromParsedAttr(Properties &prop, ::mlir::Attribute attr, ::llvm::function_ref<::mlir::InFlightDiagnostic()> emitError);
   private:
     ::mlir::StringAttr getAttributeNameForIndex(unsigned index) {
       return getAttributeNameForIndex((*this)->getName(), index);
     }
   
     static ::mlir::StringAttr getAttributeNameForIndex(::mlir::OperationName name, unsigned index) {
       assert(index < 3 && "invalid attribute index");
       assert(name.getStringRef() == getOperationName() && "invalid operation name");
       assert(name.isRegistered() && "Operation isn't registered, missing a "
             "dependent dialect loading?");
       return name.getAttributeNames()[index];
     }
   
   public:
     Value getInductionVariable();
     int64_t getTripCount();
   };
   
   } // namespace mlir::emithls
