   class FuncOpGenericAdaptorBase {
   public:
     struct Properties {
       using arg_attrsTy = ::mlir::ArrayAttr;
       arg_attrsTy arg_attrs;
   
       auto getArgAttrs() const {
         auto &propStorage = this->arg_attrs;
         return ::llvm::dyn_cast_or_null<::mlir::ArrayAttr>(propStorage);
       }
       void setArgAttrs(const ::mlir::ArrayAttr &propValue) {
         this->arg_attrs = propValue;
       }
       using function_typeTy = ::mlir::TypeAttr;
       function_typeTy function_type;
   
       auto getFunctionType() const {
         auto &propStorage = this->function_type;
         return ::llvm::cast<::mlir::TypeAttr>(propStorage);
       }
       void setFunctionType(const ::mlir::TypeAttr &propValue) {
         this->function_type = propValue;
       }
       using res_attrsTy = ::mlir::ArrayAttr;
       res_attrsTy res_attrs;
   
       auto getResAttrs() const {
         auto &propStorage = this->res_attrs;
         return ::llvm::dyn_cast_or_null<::mlir::ArrayAttr>(propStorage);
       }
       void setResAttrs(const ::mlir::ArrayAttr &propValue) {
         this->res_attrs = propValue;
       }
       using sym_nameTy = ::mlir::StringAttr;
       sym_nameTy sym_name;
   
       auto getSymName() const {
         auto &propStorage = this->sym_name;
         return ::llvm::cast<::mlir::StringAttr>(propStorage);
       }
       void setSymName(const ::mlir::StringAttr &propValue) {
         this->sym_name = propValue;
       }
       bool operator==(const Properties &rhs) const {
         return 
           rhs.arg_attrs == this->arg_attrs &&
           rhs.function_type == this->function_type &&
           rhs.res_attrs == this->res_attrs &&
           rhs.sym_name == this->sym_name &&
           true;
       }
       bool operator!=(const Properties &rhs) const {
         return !(*this == rhs);
       }
     };
   protected:
     ::mlir::DictionaryAttr odsAttrs;
     ::std::optional<::mlir::OperationName> odsOpName;
     Properties properties;
     ::mlir::RegionRange odsRegions;
   public:
     FuncOpGenericAdaptorBase(::mlir::DictionaryAttr attrs, const Properties &properties, ::mlir::RegionRange regions = {}) : odsAttrs(attrs), properties(properties), odsRegions(regions) {  if (odsAttrs)
         odsOpName.emplace("emithls.func", odsAttrs.getContext());
     }
   
     FuncOpGenericAdaptorBase(FuncOp op);
   
     std::pair<unsigned, unsigned> getODSOperandIndexAndLength(unsigned index, unsigned odsOperandsSize) {
       return {index, 1};
     }
   
     const Properties &getProperties() {
       return properties;
     }
   
     ::mlir::DictionaryAttr getAttributes() {
       return odsAttrs;
     }
   
     ::mlir::StringAttr getSymNameAttr() {
       auto attr = ::llvm::cast<::mlir::StringAttr>(getProperties().sym_name);
       return attr;
     }
   
     ::llvm::StringRef getSymName();
     ::mlir::TypeAttr getFunctionTypeAttr() {
       auto attr = ::llvm::cast<::mlir::TypeAttr>(getProperties().function_type);
       return attr;
     }
   
     ::mlir::FunctionType getFunctionType();
     ::mlir::ArrayAttr getArgAttrsAttr() {
       auto attr = ::llvm::dyn_cast_or_null<::mlir::ArrayAttr>(getProperties().arg_attrs);
       return attr;
     }
   
     ::std::optional< ::mlir::ArrayAttr > getArgAttrs();
     ::mlir::ArrayAttr getResAttrsAttr() {
       auto attr = ::llvm::dyn_cast_or_null<::mlir::ArrayAttr>(getProperties().res_attrs);
       return attr;
     }
   
     ::std::optional< ::mlir::ArrayAttr > getResAttrs();
     ::mlir::Region &getBody() {
       return *odsRegions[0];
     }
   
     ::mlir::RegionRange getRegions() {
       return odsRegions;
     }
   
   };
   
   } // namespace detail

   class FuncOp : public ::mlir::Op<FuncOp, ::mlir::OpTrait::OneRegion, ::mlir::OpTrait::ZeroResults, ::mlir::OpTrait::ZeroSuccessors, ::mlir::OpTrait::ZeroOperands, ::mlir::OpTrait::NoTerminator, ::mlir::OpTrait::OpInvariants, ::mlir::BytecodeOpInterface::Trait, ::mlir::OpTrait::AutomaticAllocationScope, ::mlir::SymbolOpInterface::Trait, ::mlir::ArgAndResultAttrsOpInterface::Trait, ::mlir::CallableOpInterface::Trait, ::mlir::FunctionOpInterface::Trait, ::mlir::OpTrait::IsIsolatedFromAbove> {

     void writeProperties(::mlir::DialectBytecodeWriter &writer);
     ::mlir::StringAttr getSymNameAttr() {
       return ::llvm::cast<::mlir::StringAttr>(getProperties().sym_name);
     }

     }

     void setArgAttrsAttr(::mlir::ArrayAttr attr) {
       getProperties().arg_attrs = attr;
     }
   
     void setResAttrsAttr(::mlir::ArrayAttr attr) {
       getProperties().res_attrs = attr;
     }

     static void build(::mlir::OpBuilder &odsBuilder, ::mlir::OperationState &odsState, ::mlir::TypeRange resultTypes, ::mlir::StringAttr sym_name, ::mlir::TypeAttr function_type, /*optional*/::mlir::ArrayAttr arg_attrs, /*optional*/::mlir::ArrayAttr res_attrs);
     static FuncOp create(::mlir::OpBuilder &builder, ::mlir::Location location, ::mlir::TypeRange resultTypes, ::mlir::StringAttr sym_name, ::mlir::TypeAttr function_type, /*optional*/::mlir::ArrayAttr arg_attrs, /*optional*/::mlir::ArrayAttr res_attrs);

     static void build(::mlir::OpBuilder &odsBuilder, ::mlir::OperationState &odsState, ::llvm::StringRef sym_name, ::mlir::FunctionType function_type, /*optional*/::mlir::ArrayAttr arg_attrs, /*optional*/::mlir::ArrayAttr res_attrs);

     static FuncOp create(::mlir::ImplicitLocOpBuilder &builder, ::llvm::StringRef sym_name, ::mlir::FunctionType function_type, /*optional*/::mlir::ArrayAttr arg_attrs, /*optional*/::mlir::ArrayAttr res_attrs);
     static void build(::mlir::OpBuilder &odsBuilder, ::mlir::OperationState &odsState, ::mlir::TypeRange resultTypes, ::llvm::StringRef sym_name, ::mlir::FunctionType function_type, /*optional*/::mlir::ArrayAttr arg_attrs, /*optional*/::mlir::ArrayAttr res_attrs);
     static FuncOp create(::mlir::OpBuilder &builder, ::mlir::Location location, ::mlir::TypeRange resultTypes, ::llvm::StringRef sym_name, ::mlir::FunctionType function_type, /*optional*/::mlir::ArrayAttr arg_attrs, /*optional*/::mlir::ArrayAttr res_attrs);
     static FuncOp create(::mlir::ImplicitLocOpBuilder &builder, ::mlir::TypeRange resultTypes, ::llvm::StringRef sym_name, ::mlir::FunctionType function_type, /*optional*/::mlir::ArrayAttr arg_attrs, /*optional*/::mlir::ArrayAttr res_attrs);
     static void build(::mlir::OpBuilder &, ::mlir::OperationState &odsState, ::mlir::TypeRange resultTypes, ::mlir::ValueRange operands, ::llvm::ArrayRef<::mlir::NamedAttribute> attributes = {});
     static FuncOp create(::mlir::OpBuilder &builder, ::mlir::Location location, ::mlir::TypeRange resultTypes, ::mlir::ValueRange operands, ::llvm::ArrayRef<::mlir::NamedAttribute> attributes = {});
     static FuncOp create(::mlir::ImplicitLocOpBuilder &builder, ::mlir::TypeRange resultTypes, ::mlir::ValueRange operands, ::llvm::ArrayRef<::mlir::NamedAttribute> attributes = {});

     static FuncOp create(::mlir::OpBuilder &builder, ::mlir::Location location, ::mlir::TypeRange resultTypes, ::mlir::ValueRange operands, const Properties &properties, ::llvm::ArrayRef<::mlir::NamedAttribute> discardableAttributes = {});
     static FuncOp create(::mlir::ImplicitLocOpBuilder &builder, ::mlir::TypeRange resultTypes, ::mlir::ValueRange operands, const Properties &properties, ::llvm::ArrayRef<::mlir::NamedAttribute> discardableAttributes = {});
     static ::mlir::ParseResult parse(::mlir::OpAsmParser &parser, ::mlir::OperationState &result);
     void print(::mlir::OpAsmPrinter &p);
     ::llvm::LogicalResult verifyInvariantsImpl();
     ::llvm::LogicalResult verifyInvariants();
     ::llvm::LogicalResult verify();
     static ::llvm::LogicalResult setPropertiesFromParsedAttr(Properties &prop, ::mlir::Attribute attr, ::llvm::function_ref<::mlir::InFlightDiagnostic()> emitError);
   private:
     ::mlir::StringAttr getAttributeNameForIndex(unsigned index) {
       return getAttributeNameForIndex((*this)->getName(), index);
     }
   
     static ::mlir::StringAttr getAttributeNameForIndex(::mlir::OperationName name, unsigned index) {
       assert(index < 4 && "invalid attribute index");
       assert(name.getStringRef() == getOperationName() && "invalid operation name");
       assert(name.isRegistered() && "Operation isn't registered, missing a "
             "dependent dialect loading?");
       return name.getAttributeNames()[index];
     }
   
   public:
     //===------------------------------------------------------------------===//
     // FunctionOpInterface Methods
     //===------------------------------------------------------------------===//
   
     ::mlir::Region *getCallableRegion();
     ArrayRef<Type> getArgumentTypes();
     ArrayRef<Type> getResultTypes();
   };

   class HelperAccumulateOpGenericAdaptorBase {
   public:
     struct Properties {
       using op_codeTy = ::mlir::emithls::FusedOperatorAttr;
       op_codeTy op_code;
   
       auto getOpCode() const {
         auto &propStorage = this->op_code;
         return ::llvm::cast<::mlir::emithls::FusedOperatorAttr>(propStorage);
       }
       void setOpCode(const ::mlir::emithls::FusedOperatorAttr &propValue) {
         this->op_code = propValue;
       }
       bool operator==(const Properties &rhs) const {
         return 
           rhs.op_code == this->op_code &&
           true;
       }
       bool operator!=(const Properties &rhs) const {
         return !(*this == rhs);
       }
     };
   protected:
     ::mlir::DictionaryAttr odsAttrs;
     ::std::optional<::mlir::OperationName> odsOpName;
     Properties properties;
     ::mlir::RegionRange odsRegions;
   public:
     HelperAccumulateOpGenericAdaptorBase(::mlir::DictionaryAttr attrs, const Properties &properties, ::mlir::RegionRange regions = {}) : odsAttrs(attrs), properties(properties), odsRegions(regions) {  if (odsAttrs)
         odsOpName.emplace("emithls.helper.accumulate", odsAttrs.getContext());
     }
   
     HelperAccumulateOpGenericAdaptorBase(HelperAccumulateOp op);
   
     std::pair<unsigned, unsigned> getODSOperandIndexAndLength(unsigned index, unsigned odsOperandsSize);
     const Properties &getProperties() {
       return properties;
     }
   
     ::mlir::DictionaryAttr getAttributes() {
       return odsAttrs;
     }
   
     ::mlir::emithls::FusedOperatorAttr getOpCodeAttr() {
       auto attr = ::llvm::cast<::mlir::emithls::FusedOperatorAttr>(getProperties().op_code);
       return attr;
     }
   
     ::mlir::emithls::FusedOperator getOpCode();
   };
   
   } // namespace detail
   template <typename RangeT>
   class HelperAccumulateOpGenericAdaptor : public detail::HelperAccumulateOpGenericAdaptorBase {
     using ValueT = ::llvm::detail::ValueOfRange<RangeT>;
     using Base = detail::HelperAccumulateOpGenericAdaptorBase;
     static constexpr int odsIndex_accu_ref = 0;
     static constexpr int odsIndex_value = 1;
     static constexpr int odsIndex_indices = 2;
   public:
     HelperAccumulateOpGenericAdaptor(RangeT values, ::mlir::DictionaryAttr attrs, const Properties &properties, ::mlir::RegionRange regions = {}) : Base(attrs, properties, regions), odsOperands(values) {}
   
     HelperAccumulateOpGenericAdaptor(RangeT values, ::mlir::DictionaryAttr attrs, ::mlir::PropertyRef properties, ::mlir::RegionRange regions = {}) : HelperAccumulateOpGenericAdaptor(values, attrs, (properties ? *properties.as<Properties *>() : Properties{}), regions) {}
   
     HelperAccumulateOpGenericAdaptor(RangeT values, ::mlir::DictionaryAttr attrs = nullptr) : HelperAccumulateOpGenericAdaptor(values, attrs, Properties{}, {}) {}
   
     HelperAccumulateOpGenericAdaptor(RangeT values, const HelperAccumulateOpGenericAdaptorBase &base) : Base(base), odsOperands(values) {}
   
     template <typename LateInst = HelperAccumulateOp, typename = std::enable_if_t<std::is_same_v<LateInst, HelperAccumulateOp>>>
     HelperAccumulateOpGenericAdaptor(RangeT values, LateInst op) : Base(op), odsOperands(values) {}
   
     std::pair<unsigned, unsigned> getODSOperandIndexAndLength(unsigned index) {
       return Base::getODSOperandIndexAndLength(index, odsOperands.size());
     }
   
     RangeT getODSOperands(unsigned index) {
       auto valueRange = getODSOperandIndexAndLength(index);
       return {std::next(odsOperands.begin(), valueRange.first),
                std::next(odsOperands.begin(), valueRange.first + valueRange.second)};
     }
   
     ValueT getAccuRef() {
       return (*getODSOperands(0).begin());
     }
   
     ValueT getValue() {
       return (*getODSOperands(1).begin());
     }
   
     RangeT getIndices() {
       return getODSOperands(2);
     }
   
     RangeT getOperands() {
       return odsOperands;
     }
   
   private:
     RangeT odsOperands;
   };
   class HelperAccumulateOpAdaptor : public HelperAccumulateOpGenericAdaptor<::mlir::ValueRange> {
   public:
     using HelperAccumulateOpGenericAdaptor::HelperAccumulateOpGenericAdaptor;
     HelperAccumulateOpAdaptor(HelperAccumulateOp op);
   
     ::llvm::LogicalResult verify(::mlir::Location loc);
   };
   class HelperAccumulateOp : public ::mlir::Op<HelperAccumulateOp, ::mlir::OpTrait::ZeroRegions, ::mlir::OpTrait::ZeroResults, ::mlir::OpTrait::ZeroSuccessors, ::mlir::OpTrait::AtLeastNOperands<2>::Impl, ::mlir::OpTrait::HasParent<ForOp, affine::AffineForOp>::Impl, ::mlir::OpTrait::OpInvariants, ::mlir::BytecodeOpInterface::Trait> {
   public:
     using Op::Op;
     using Op::print;
     using Adaptor = HelperAccumulateOpAdaptor;
     template <typename RangeT>
     using GenericAdaptor = HelperAccumulateOpGenericAdaptor<RangeT>;
     using FoldAdaptor = GenericAdaptor<::llvm::ArrayRef<::mlir::Attribute>>;
     static constexpr int odsIndex_accu_ref = 0;
     static constexpr int odsIndex_value = 1;
     static constexpr int odsIndex_indices = 2;
     using Properties = FoldAdaptor::Properties;
     static ::llvm::ArrayRef<::llvm::StringRef> getAttributeNames() {
       static ::llvm::StringRef attrNames[] = {::llvm::StringRef("op_code")};
       return ::llvm::ArrayRef(attrNames);
     }
   
     ::mlir::StringAttr getOpCodeAttrName() {
       return getAttributeNameForIndex(0);
     }
   
     static ::mlir::StringAttr getOpCodeAttrName(::mlir::OperationName name) {
       return getAttributeNameForIndex(name, 0);
     }
   
     static constexpr ::llvm::StringLiteral getOperationName() {
       return ::llvm::StringLiteral("emithls.helper.accumulate");
     }
   
     std::pair<unsigned, unsigned> getODSOperandIndexAndLength(unsigned index);
     ::mlir::Operation::operand_range getODSOperands(unsigned index) {
       auto valueRange = getODSOperandIndexAndLength(index);
       return {std::next(getOperation()->operand_begin(), valueRange.first),
                std::next(getOperation()->operand_begin(), valueRange.first + valueRange.second)};
     }
   
     ::mlir::TypedValue<::mlir::Type> getAccuRef() {
       return ::llvm::cast<::mlir::TypedValue<::mlir::Type>>(*getODSOperands(0).begin());
     }
   
     ::mlir::TypedValue<::mlir::Type> getValue() {
       return ::llvm::cast<::mlir::TypedValue<::mlir::Type>>(*getODSOperands(1).begin());
     }
   
     ::mlir::Operation::operand_range getIndices() {
       return getODSOperands(2);
     }
   
     ::mlir::OpOperand &getAccuRefMutable() {
       auto range = getODSOperandIndexAndLength(0);
       return getOperation()->getOpOperand(range.first);
     }
   
     ::mlir::OpOperand &getValueMutable() {
       auto range = getODSOperandIndexAndLength(1);
       return getOperation()->getOpOperand(range.first);
     }
   
     ::mlir::MutableOperandRange getIndicesMutable();
     std::pair<unsigned, unsigned> getODSResultIndexAndLength(unsigned index) {
       return {index, 1};
     }
   
     ::mlir::Operation::result_range getODSResults(unsigned index) {
       auto valueRange = getODSResultIndexAndLength(index);
       return {std::next(getOperation()->result_begin(), valueRange.first),
                std::next(getOperation()->result_begin(), valueRange.first + valueRange.second)};
     }
   
     static ::llvm::LogicalResult setPropertiesFromAttr(Properties &prop, ::mlir::Attribute attr, ::llvm::function_ref<::mlir::InFlightDiagnostic()> emitError);
     static ::mlir::Attribute getPropertiesAsAttr(::mlir::MLIRContext *ctx, const Properties &prop);
     static llvm::hash_code computePropertiesHash(const Properties &prop);
     static std::optional<mlir::Attribute> getInherentAttr(::mlir::MLIRContext *ctx, const Properties &prop, llvm::StringRef name);
     static void setInherentAttr(Properties &prop, llvm::StringRef name, mlir::Attribute value);
     static void populateInherentAttrs(::mlir::MLIRContext *ctx, const Properties &prop, ::mlir::NamedAttrList &attrs);
     static ::llvm::LogicalResult verifyInherentAttrs(::mlir::OperationName opName, ::mlir::NamedAttrList &attrs, llvm::function_ref<::mlir::InFlightDiagnostic()> emitError);
     static ::llvm::LogicalResult readProperties(::mlir::DialectBytecodeReader &reader, ::mlir::OperationState &state);
     void writeProperties(::mlir::DialectBytecodeWriter &writer);
     ::mlir::emithls::FusedOperatorAttr getOpCodeAttr() {
       return ::llvm::cast<::mlir::emithls::FusedOperatorAttr>(getProperties().op_code);
     }
   
     ::mlir::emithls::FusedOperator getOpCode();
     void setOpCodeAttr(::mlir::emithls::FusedOperatorAttr attr) {
       getProperties().op_code = attr;
     }
   
     void setOpCode(::mlir::emithls::FusedOperator attrValue);
     static void build(::mlir::OpBuilder &odsBuilder, ::mlir::OperationState &odsState, Value accuRef, ValueRange indices, FusedOperator operatorCode, Value value);
     static HelperAccumulateOp create(::mlir::OpBuilder &builder, ::mlir::Location location, Value accuRef, ValueRange indices, FusedOperator operatorCode, Value value);
     static HelperAccumulateOp create(::mlir::ImplicitLocOpBuilder &builder, Value accuRef, ValueRange indices, FusedOperator operatorCode, Value value);
     static ::mlir::ParseResult parse(::mlir::OpAsmParser &parser, ::mlir::OperationState &result);
     void print(::mlir::OpAsmPrinter &p);
     ::llvm::LogicalResult verifyInvariantsImpl();
     ::llvm::LogicalResult verifyInvariants();
     static ::llvm::LogicalResult setPropertiesFromParsedAttr(Properties &prop, ::mlir::Attribute attr, ::llvm::function_ref<::mlir::InFlightDiagnostic()> emitError);
   private:
     ::mlir::StringAttr getAttributeNameForIndex(unsigned index) {
       return getAttributeNameForIndex((*this)->getName(), index);
     }
   
     static ::mlir::StringAttr getAttributeNameForIndex(::mlir::OperationName name, unsigned index) {
       assert(index < 1 && "invalid attribute index");
       assert(name.getStringRef() == getOperationName() && "invalid operation name");
       assert(name.isRegistered() && "Operation isn't registered, missing a "
             "dependent dialect loading?");
       return name.getAttributeNames()[index];
     }

   class HelperLineBufferOpGenericAdaptorBase {
   public:
     struct Properties {
       using num_chanTy = ::mlir::ArrayAttr;
       num_chanTy num_chan;
   
       auto getNumChan() const {
         auto &propStorage = this->num_chan;
         return ::llvm::cast<::mlir::ArrayAttr>(propStorage);
       }
       void setNumChan(const ::mlir::ArrayAttr &propValue) {
         this->num_chan = propValue;
       }
       using num_lineTy = ::mlir::IntegerAttr;
       num_lineTy num_line;
   
       auto getNumLine() const {
         auto &propStorage = this->num_line;
         return ::llvm::cast<::mlir::IntegerAttr>(propStorage);
       }
       void setNumLine(const ::mlir::IntegerAttr &propValue) {
         this->num_line = propValue;
       }
       bool operator==(const Properties &rhs) const {
         return 
           rhs.num_chan == this->num_chan &&
           rhs.num_line == this->num_line &&
           true;
       }
       bool operator!=(const Properties &rhs) const {
         return !(*this == rhs);
       }
     };
   protected:
     ::mlir::DictionaryAttr odsAttrs;
     ::std::optional<::mlir::OperationName> odsOpName;
     Properties properties;
     ::mlir::RegionRange odsRegions;
   public:
     HelperLineBufferOpGenericAdaptorBase(::mlir::DictionaryAttr attrs, const Properties &properties, ::mlir::RegionRange regions = {}) : odsAttrs(attrs), properties(properties), odsRegions(regions) {  if (odsAttrs)
         odsOpName.emplace("emithls.helper.linebuf", odsAttrs.getContext());
     }
   
     HelperLineBufferOpGenericAdaptorBase(HelperLineBufferOp op);
   
     std::pair<unsigned, unsigned> getODSOperandIndexAndLength(unsigned index, unsigned odsOperandsSize);
     const Properties &getProperties() {
       return properties;
     }
   
     ::mlir::DictionaryAttr getAttributes() {
       return odsAttrs;
     }
   
     ::mlir::ArrayAttr getNumChanAttr() {
       auto attr = ::llvm::cast<::mlir::ArrayAttr>(getProperties().num_chan);
       return attr;
     }
   
     ::mlir::ArrayAttr getNumChan();
     ::mlir::IntegerAttr getNumLineAttr() {
       auto attr = ::llvm::cast<::mlir::IntegerAttr>(getProperties().num_line);
       return attr;
     }
   
     uint32_t getNumLine();
   };
   
   } // namespace detail
   template <typename RangeT>
   class HelperLineBufferOpGenericAdaptor : public detail::HelperLineBufferOpGenericAdaptorBase {
     using ValueT = ::llvm::detail::ValueOfRange<RangeT>;
     using Base = detail::HelperLineBufferOpGenericAdaptorBase;
     static constexpr int odsIndex_token_ref = 0;
     static constexpr int odsIndex_index = 1;
   public:
     HelperLineBufferOpGenericAdaptor(RangeT values, ::mlir::DictionaryAttr attrs, const Properties &properties, ::mlir::RegionRange regions = {}) : Base(attrs, properties, regions), odsOperands(values) {}
   
     HelperLineBufferOpGenericAdaptor(RangeT values, ::mlir::DictionaryAttr attrs, ::mlir::PropertyRef properties, ::mlir::RegionRange regions = {}) : HelperLineBufferOpGenericAdaptor(values, attrs, (properties ? *properties.as<Properties *>() : Properties{}), regions) {}
   
     HelperLineBufferOpGenericAdaptor(RangeT values, ::mlir::DictionaryAttr attrs = nullptr) : HelperLineBufferOpGenericAdaptor(values, attrs, Properties{}, {}) {}
   
     HelperLineBufferOpGenericAdaptor(RangeT values, const HelperLineBufferOpGenericAdaptorBase &base) : Base(base), odsOperands(values) {}
   
     template <typename LateInst = HelperLineBufferOp, typename = std::enable_if_t<std::is_same_v<LateInst, HelperLineBufferOp>>>
     HelperLineBufferOpGenericAdaptor(RangeT values, LateInst op) : Base(op), odsOperands(values) {}
   
     std::pair<unsigned, unsigned> getODSOperandIndexAndLength(unsigned index) {
       return Base::getODSOperandIndexAndLength(index, odsOperands.size());
     }
   
     RangeT getODSOperands(unsigned index) {
       auto valueRange = getODSOperandIndexAndLength(index);
       return {std::next(odsOperands.begin(), valueRange.first),
                std::next(odsOperands.begin(), valueRange.first + valueRange.second)};
     }
   
     ValueT getTokenRef() {
       return (*getODSOperands(0).begin());
     }
   
     ValueT getIndex() {
       auto operands = getODSOperands(1);
       return operands.empty() ? ValueT{} : (*operands.begin());
     }
   
     RangeT getOperands() {
       return odsOperands;
     }
   
   private:
     RangeT odsOperands;
   };

   };
   class HelperLineBufferOp : public ::mlir::Op<HelperLineBufferOp, ::mlir::OpTrait::ZeroRegions, ::mlir::OpTrait::OneResult, ::mlir::OpTrait::OneTypedResult<::mlir::Type>::Impl, ::mlir::OpTrait::ZeroSuccessors, ::mlir::OpTrait::AtLeastNOperands<1>::Impl, ::mlir::OpTrait::HasParent<ForOp, affine::AffineForOp>::Impl, ::mlir::OpTrait::OpInvariants, ::mlir::BytecodeOpInterface::Trait, ::mlir::OpAsmOpInterface::Trait> {

     static constexpr int odsIndex_index = 1;

     using ::mlir::OpAsmOpInterface::Trait<HelperLineBufferOp>::getAsmBlockNames;
     using ::mlir::OpAsmOpInterface::Trait<HelperLineBufferOp>::getDefaultDialect;
     static ::llvm::ArrayRef<::llvm::StringRef> getAttributeNames() {
       static ::llvm::StringRef attrNames[] = {::llvm::StringRef("num_chan"), ::llvm::StringRef("num_line")};
       return ::llvm::ArrayRef(attrNames);
     }
   
     ::mlir::StringAttr getNumChanAttrName() {
       return getAttributeNameForIndex(0);

     }

       return getAttributeNameForIndex(1);
     }

       return getAttributeNameForIndex(name, 1);

       return ::llvm::StringLiteral("emithls.helper.linebuf");
     }

                std::next(getOperation()->operand_begin(), valueRange.first + valueRange.second)};

       return ::llvm::cast<::mlir::TypedValue<::mlir::Type>>(*getODSOperands(0).begin());
     }

       return operands.empty() ? ::mlir::TypedValue<::mlir::IndexType>{} : ::llvm::cast<::mlir::TypedValue<::mlir::IndexType>>(*operands.begin());
     }

       auto range = getODSOperandIndexAndLength(0);
       return getOperation()->getOpOperand(range.first);
     }

     std::pair<unsigned, unsigned> getODSResultIndexAndLength(unsigned index) {
       return {index, 1};
     }
   
     ::mlir::Operation::result_range getODSResults(unsigned index) {
       auto valueRange = getODSResultIndexAndLength(index);
       return {std::next(getOperation()->result_begin(), valueRange.first),
                std::next(getOperation()->result_begin(), valueRange.first + valueRange.second)};
     }
   
     ::mlir::TypedValue<::mlir::Type> getBufRef() {
       return ::llvm::cast<::mlir::TypedValue<::mlir::Type>>(*getODSResults(0).begin());
     }

     static ::mlir::Attribute getPropertiesAsAttr(::mlir::MLIRContext *ctx, const Properties &prop);

     }

     }
   
     void setNumLine(uint32_t attrValue);
     static void build(::mlir::OpBuilder &odsBuilder, ::mlir::OperationState &odsState, Value tokenRef, Value index, Type linebufType, ArrayRef<int32_t> numChan, int32_t numLine);
     static HelperLineBufferOp create(::mlir::OpBuilder &builder, ::mlir::Location location, Value tokenRef, Value index, Type linebufType, ArrayRef<int32_t> numChan, int32_t numLine);
     static HelperLineBufferOp create(::mlir::ImplicitLocOpBuilder &builder, Value tokenRef, Value index, Type linebufType, ArrayRef<int32_t> numChan, int32_t numLine);
     static ::mlir::ParseResult parse(::mlir::OpAsmParser &parser, ::mlir::OperationState &result);
     void print(::mlir::OpAsmPrinter &p);
     ::llvm::LogicalResult verifyInvariantsImpl();
     ::llvm::LogicalResult verifyInvariants();
     void getAsmResultNames(::mlir::OpAsmSetValueNameFn setNameFn);
     static ::llvm::LogicalResult setPropertiesFromParsedAttr(Properties &prop, ::mlir::Attribute attr, ::llvm::function_ref<::mlir::InFlightDiagnostic()> emitError);
   private:
     ::mlir::StringAttr getAttributeNameForIndex(unsigned index) {
       return getAttributeNameForIndex((*this)->getName(), index);
     }
   
     static ::mlir::StringAttr getAttributeNameForIndex(::mlir::OperationName name, unsigned index) {
       assert(index < 2 && "invalid attribute index");
       assert(name.getStringRef() == getOperationName() && "invalid operation name");
       assert(name.isRegistered() && "Operation isn't registered, missing a "
             "dependent dialect loading?");
       return name.getAttributeNames()[index];
     }
   
   public:
   };
   
   } // namespace mlir::emithls

   template <typename RangeT>
   class HelperWindowOpGenericAdaptor : public detail::HelperWindowOpGenericAdaptorBase {
     using ValueT = ::llvm::detail::ValueOfRange<RangeT>;
     using Base = detail::HelperWindowOpGenericAdaptorBase;
     static constexpr int odsIndex_buf_ref = 0;
     static constexpr int odsIndex_indices = 1;
   public:
     HelperWindowOpGenericAdaptor(RangeT values, ::mlir::DictionaryAttr attrs, const Properties &properties, ::mlir::RegionRange regions = {}) : Base(attrs, properties, regions), odsOperands(values) {}
   
     HelperWindowOpGenericAdaptor(RangeT values, ::mlir::DictionaryAttr attrs, ::mlir::PropertyRef properties, ::mlir::RegionRange regions = {}) : HelperWindowOpGenericAdaptor(values, attrs, (properties ? *properties.as<Properties *>() : Properties{}), regions) {}
   
     HelperWindowOpGenericAdaptor(RangeT values, ::mlir::DictionaryAttr attrs = nullptr) : HelperWindowOpGenericAdaptor(values, attrs, Properties{}, {}) {}
   
     HelperWindowOpGenericAdaptor(RangeT values, const HelperWindowOpGenericAdaptorBase &base) : Base(base), odsOperands(values) {}
   
     template <typename LateInst = HelperWindowOp, typename = std::enable_if_t<std::is_same_v<LateInst, HelperWindowOp>>>
     HelperWindowOpGenericAdaptor(RangeT values, LateInst op) : Base(op), odsOperands(values) {}
   
     std::pair<unsigned, unsigned> getODSOperandIndexAndLength(unsigned index) {
       return Base::getODSOperandIndexAndLength(index, odsOperands.size());
     }
   
     RangeT getODSOperands(unsigned index) {
       auto valueRange = getODSOperandIndexAndLength(index);
       return {std::next(odsOperands.begin(), valueRange.first),
                std::next(odsOperands.begin(), valueRange.first + valueRange.second)};
     }
   
     ValueT getBufRef() {
       return (*getODSOperands(0).begin());
     }
   
     RangeT getIndices() {
       return getODSOperands(1);
     }
   
     RangeT getOperands() {
       return odsOperands;
     }
   
   private:
     RangeT odsOperands;
   };
   class HelperWindowOpAdaptor : public HelperWindowOpGenericAdaptor<::mlir::ValueRange> {
   public:
     using HelperWindowOpGenericAdaptor::HelperWindowOpGenericAdaptor;
     HelperWindowOpAdaptor(HelperWindowOp op);
   
     ::llvm::LogicalResult verify(::mlir::Location loc);
   };
   class HelperWindowOp : public ::mlir::Op<HelperWindowOp, ::mlir::OpTrait::ZeroRegions, ::mlir::OpTrait::OneResult, ::mlir::OpTrait::OneTypedResult<::mlir::Type>::Impl, ::mlir::OpTrait::ZeroSuccessors, ::mlir::OpTrait::AtLeastNOperands<1>::Impl, ::mlir::OpTrait::HasParent<ForOp, affine::AffineForOp>::Impl, ::mlir::OpTrait::OpInvariants, ::mlir::OpAsmOpInterface::Trait> {
   public:
     using Op::Op;
     using Op::print;
     using Adaptor = HelperWindowOpAdaptor;
     template <typename RangeT>
     using GenericAdaptor = HelperWindowOpGenericAdaptor<RangeT>;
     using FoldAdaptor = GenericAdaptor<::llvm::ArrayRef<::mlir::Attribute>>;
     static constexpr int odsIndex_buf_ref = 0;
     static constexpr int odsIndex_indices = 1;
     using Properties = FoldAdaptor::Properties;
     using ::mlir::OpAsmOpInterface::Trait<HelperWindowOp>::getAsmBlockArgumentNames;
     using ::mlir::OpAsmOpInterface::Trait<HelperWindowOp>::getAsmBlockNames;
     using ::mlir::OpAsmOpInterface::Trait<HelperWindowOp>::getDefaultDialect;
     static ::llvm::ArrayRef<::llvm::StringRef> getAttributeNames() {
       return {};
     }
   
     static constexpr ::llvm::StringLiteral getOperationName() {
       return ::llvm::StringLiteral("emithls.helper.window");
     }
   
     std::pair<unsigned, unsigned> getODSOperandIndexAndLength(unsigned index);
     ::mlir::Operation::operand_range getODSOperands(unsigned index) {
       auto valueRange = getODSOperandIndexAndLength(index);
       return {std::next(getOperation()->operand_begin(), valueRange.first),
                std::next(getOperation()->operand_begin(), valueRange.first + valueRange.second)};
     }
   
     ::mlir::TypedValue<::mlir::Type> getBufRef() {
       return ::llvm::cast<::mlir::TypedValue<::mlir::Type>>(*getODSOperands(0).begin());
     }
   
     ::mlir::Operation::operand_range getIndices() {
       return getODSOperands(1);
     }
   
     ::mlir::OpOperand &getBufRefMutable() {
       auto range = getODSOperandIndexAndLength(0);
       return getOperation()->getOpOperand(range.first);
     }
   
     ::mlir::MutableOperandRange getIndicesMutable();
     std::pair<unsigned, unsigned> getODSResultIndexAndLength(unsigned index) {
       return {index, 1};
     }
   
     ::mlir::Operation::result_range getODSResults(unsigned index) {
       auto valueRange = getODSResultIndexAndLength(index);
       return {std::next(getOperation()->result_begin(), valueRange.first),
                std::next(getOperation()->result_begin(), valueRange.first + valueRange.second)};
     }
   
     ::mlir::TypedValue<::mlir::Type> getWinRef() {
       return ::llvm::cast<::mlir::TypedValue<::mlir::Type>>(*getODSResults(0).begin());
     }
   
     static ::llvm::LogicalResult setPropertiesFromAttr(Properties &prop, ::mlir::Attribute attr, ::llvm::function_ref<::mlir::InFlightDiagnostic()> emitError);
     static ::mlir::Attribute getPropertiesAsAttr(::mlir::MLIRContext *ctx, const Properties &prop);
     static llvm::hash_code computePropertiesHash(const Properties &prop);
     static std::optional<mlir::Attribute> getInherentAttr(::mlir::MLIRContext *ctx, const Properties &prop, llvm::StringRef name);
     static void setInherentAttr(Properties &prop, llvm::StringRef name, mlir::Attribute value);
     static void populateInherentAttrs(::mlir::MLIRContext *ctx, const Properties &prop, ::mlir::NamedAttrList &attrs);
     static ::llvm::LogicalResult verifyInherentAttrs(::mlir::OperationName opName, ::mlir::NamedAttrList &attrs, llvm::function_ref<::mlir::InFlightDiagnostic()> emitError);
     static void build(::mlir::OpBuilder &odsBuilder, ::mlir::OperationState &odsState, Value linebuf, ValueRange indices, Type windowType);
     static HelperWindowOp create(::mlir::OpBuilder &builder, ::mlir::Location location, Value linebuf, ValueRange indices, Type windowType);
     static HelperWindowOp create(::mlir::ImplicitLocOpBuilder &builder, Value linebuf, ValueRange indices, Type windowType);
     static ::mlir::ParseResult parse(::mlir::OpAsmParser &parser, ::mlir::OperationState &result);
     void print(::mlir::OpAsmPrinter &p);
     ::llvm::LogicalResult verifyInvariantsImpl();

   };

   //===----------------------------------------------------------------------===//
   // ::mlir::emithls::IncludeOp declarations
   //===----------------------------------------------------------------------===//
   
   namespace detail {
   
   class IncludeOpGenericAdaptorBase {
   public:
     struct Properties {
       using headerTy = ::mlir::StringAttr;
       headerTy header;
   
       auto getHeader() const {
         auto &propStorage = this->header;
         return ::llvm::cast<::mlir::StringAttr>(propStorage);
       }
       void setHeader(const ::mlir::StringAttr &propValue) {
         this->header = propValue;
       }
       bool operator==(const Properties &rhs) const {
         return 
           rhs.header == this->header &&
           true;
       }
       bool operator!=(const Properties &rhs) const {
         return !(*this == rhs);
       }
     };
   protected:
     ::mlir::DictionaryAttr odsAttrs;
     ::std::optional<::mlir::OperationName> odsOpName;
     Properties properties;
     ::mlir::RegionRange odsRegions;
   public:
     IncludeOpGenericAdaptorBase(::mlir::DictionaryAttr attrs, const Properties &properties, ::mlir::RegionRange regions = {}) : odsAttrs(attrs), properties(properties), odsRegions(regions) {  if (odsAttrs)
         odsOpName.emplace("emithls.include", odsAttrs.getContext());
     }
   
     IncludeOpGenericAdaptorBase(IncludeOp op);
   
     std::pair<unsigned, unsigned> getODSOperandIndexAndLength(unsigned index, unsigned odsOperandsSize) {
       return {index, 1};
     }
   
     const Properties &getProperties() {
       return properties;
     }
   
     ::mlir::DictionaryAttr getAttributes() {
       return odsAttrs;
     }
   
     ::mlir::StringAttr getHeaderAttr() {
       auto attr = ::llvm::cast<::mlir::StringAttr>(getProperties().header);
       return attr;
     }
   
     ::llvm::StringRef getHeader();
   };
   
   } // namespace detail
   template <typename RangeT>

   class IncludeOp : public ::mlir::Op<IncludeOp, ::mlir::OpTrait::ZeroRegions, ::mlir::OpTrait::ZeroResults, ::mlir::OpTrait::ZeroSuccessors, ::mlir::OpTrait::ZeroOperands, ::mlir::OpTrait::HasParent<ModuleOp>::Impl, ::mlir::OpTrait::OpInvariants, ::mlir::BytecodeOpInterface::Trait> {

     static ::llvm::ArrayRef<::llvm::StringRef> getAttributeNames() {
       static ::llvm::StringRef attrNames[] = {::llvm::StringRef("header")};
       return ::llvm::ArrayRef(attrNames);
     }

       return getAttributeNameForIndex(0);
     }
   
     static ::mlir::StringAttr getHeaderAttrName(::mlir::OperationName name) {
       return getAttributeNameForIndex(name, 0);
     }
   
     static constexpr ::llvm::StringLiteral getOperationName() {
       return ::llvm::StringLiteral("emithls.include");
     }
   
     std::pair<unsigned, unsigned> getODSOperandIndexAndLength(unsigned index) {
       return {index, 1};
     }

       auto valueRange = getODSOperandIndexAndLength(index);
       return {std::next(getOperation()->operand_begin(), valueRange.first),
                std::next(getOperation()->operand_begin(), valueRange.first + valueRange.second)};
     }

       return {index, 1};
     }

       return {std::next(getOperation()->result_begin(), valueRange.first),
                std::next(getOperation()->result_begin(), valueRange.first + valueRange.second)};
     }
   
     static ::llvm::LogicalResult setPropertiesFromAttr(Properties &prop, ::mlir::Attribute attr, ::llvm::function_ref<::mlir::InFlightDiagnostic()> emitError);

     static void setInherentAttr(Properties &prop, llvm::StringRef name, mlir::Attribute value);

     static ::llvm::LogicalResult verifyInherentAttrs(::mlir::OperationName opName, ::mlir::NamedAttrList &attrs, llvm::function_ref<::mlir::InFlightDiagnostic()> emitError);
     static ::llvm::LogicalResult readProperties(::mlir::DialectBytecodeReader &reader, ::mlir::OperationState &state);
     void writeProperties(::mlir::DialectBytecodeWriter &writer);
     ::mlir::StringAttr getHeaderAttr() {
       return ::llvm::cast<::mlir::StringAttr>(getProperties().header);
     }
   
     ::llvm::StringRef getHeader();
     void setHeaderAttr(::mlir::StringAttr attr) {
       getProperties().header = attr;
     }
   
     void setHeader(::llvm::StringRef attrValue);
     static void build(::mlir::OpBuilder &odsBuilder, ::mlir::OperationState &odsState, StringRef header);
     static IncludeOp create(::mlir::OpBuilder &builder, ::mlir::Location location, StringRef header);
     static IncludeOp create(::mlir::ImplicitLocOpBuilder &builder, StringRef header);
     static ::mlir::ParseResult parse(::mlir::OpAsmParser &parser, ::mlir::OperationState &result);
     void print(::mlir::OpAsmPrinter &p);
     ::llvm::LogicalResult verifyInvariantsImpl();
     ::llvm::LogicalResult verifyInvariants();
     ::llvm::LogicalResult verify();
     static void getCanonicalizationPatterns(::mlir::RewritePatternSet &results, ::mlir::MLIRContext *context);
     static ::llvm::LogicalResult setPropertiesFromParsedAttr(Properties &prop, ::mlir::Attribute attr, ::llvm::function_ref<::mlir::InFlightDiagnostic()> emitError);
   private:
     ::mlir::StringAttr getAttributeNameForIndex(unsigned index) {
       return getAttributeNameForIndex((*this)->getName(), index);
     }
   
     static ::mlir::StringAttr getAttributeNameForIndex(::mlir::OperationName name, unsigned index) {
       assert(index < 1 && "invalid attribute index");
       assert(name.getStringRef() == getOperationName() && "invalid operation name");
       assert(name.isRegistered() && "Operation isn't registered, missing a "
             "dependent dialect loading?");
       return name.getAttributeNames()[index];
     }
   
   public:
   };

   class PragmaArrayPartitionOpGenericAdaptorBase {
   public:
     struct Properties {
       using part_dimTy = ::mlir::IntegerAttr;
       part_dimTy part_dim;
   
       auto getPartDim() const {
         auto &propStorage = this->part_dim;
         return ::llvm::dyn_cast_or_null<::mlir::IntegerAttr>(propStorage);
       }
       void setPartDim(const ::mlir::IntegerAttr &propValue) {
         this->part_dim = propValue;
       }
       using part_factorTy = ::mlir::IntegerAttr;
       part_factorTy part_factor;
   
       auto getPartFactor() const {
         auto &propStorage = this->part_factor;
         return ::llvm::dyn_cast_or_null<::mlir::IntegerAttr>(propStorage);
       }
       void setPartFactor(const ::mlir::IntegerAttr &propValue) {
         this->part_factor = propValue;
       }
       using part_typeTy = ::mlir::emithls::ArrayPartitionTypeAttr;
       part_typeTy part_type;
   
       auto getPartType() const {
         auto &propStorage = this->part_type;
         return ::llvm::cast<::mlir::emithls::ArrayPartitionTypeAttr>(propStorage);
       }
       void setPartType(const ::mlir::emithls::ArrayPartitionTypeAttr &propValue) {
         this->part_type = propValue;
       }
       bool operator==(const Properties &rhs) const {
         return 
           rhs.part_dim == this->part_dim &&
           rhs.part_factor == this->part_factor &&
           rhs.part_type == this->part_type &&
           true;
       }
       bool operator!=(const Properties &rhs) const {
         return !(*this == rhs);
       }
     };
   protected:
     ::mlir::DictionaryAttr odsAttrs;
     ::std::optional<::mlir::OperationName> odsOpName;
     Properties properties;
     ::mlir::RegionRange odsRegions;
   public:
     PragmaArrayPartitionOpGenericAdaptorBase(::mlir::DictionaryAttr attrs, const Properties &properties, ::mlir::RegionRange regions = {}) : odsAttrs(attrs), properties(properties), odsRegions(regions) {  if (odsAttrs)
         odsOpName.emplace("emithls.pragma.array_partition", odsAttrs.getContext());
     }
   
     PragmaArrayPartitionOpGenericAdaptorBase(PragmaArrayPartitionOp op);
   
     std::pair<unsigned, unsigned> getODSOperandIndexAndLength(unsigned index, unsigned odsOperandsSize) {
       return {index, 1};
     }
   
     const Properties &getProperties() {
       return properties;
     }
   
     ::mlir::DictionaryAttr getAttributes() {
       return odsAttrs;
     }
   
     ::mlir::emithls::ArrayPartitionTypeAttr getPartTypeAttr() {
       auto attr = ::llvm::cast<::mlir::emithls::ArrayPartitionTypeAttr>(getProperties().part_type);
       return attr;
     }
   
     ::mlir::emithls::ArrayPartitionType getPartType();
     ::mlir::IntegerAttr getPartFactorAttr() {
       auto attr = ::llvm::dyn_cast_or_null<::mlir::IntegerAttr>(getProperties().part_factor);
       return attr;
     }
   
     ::std::optional<uint32_t> getPartFactor();
     ::mlir::IntegerAttr getPartDimAttr() {
       auto attr = ::llvm::dyn_cast_or_null<::mlir::IntegerAttr>(getProperties().part_dim);
       return attr;
     }
   
     ::std::optional<uint32_t> getPartDim();
   };

   class PragmaArrayPartitionOpGenericAdaptor : public detail::PragmaArrayPartitionOpGenericAdaptorBase {
     using ValueT = ::llvm::detail::ValueOfRange<RangeT>;

     static constexpr int odsIndex_variable = 0;
   public:
     PragmaArrayPartitionOpGenericAdaptor(RangeT values, ::mlir::DictionaryAttr attrs, const Properties &properties, ::mlir::RegionRange regions = {}) : Base(attrs, properties, regions), odsOperands(values) {}
   
     PragmaArrayPartitionOpGenericAdaptor(RangeT values, ::mlir::DictionaryAttr attrs, ::mlir::PropertyRef properties, ::mlir::RegionRange regions = {}) : PragmaArrayPartitionOpGenericAdaptor(values, attrs, (properties ? *properties.as<Properties *>() : Properties{}), regions) {}
   
     PragmaArrayPartitionOpGenericAdaptor(RangeT values, ::mlir::DictionaryAttr attrs = nullptr) : PragmaArrayPartitionOpGenericAdaptor(values, attrs, Properties{}, {}) {}
   
     PragmaArrayPartitionOpGenericAdaptor(RangeT values, const PragmaArrayPartitionOpGenericAdaptorBase &base) : Base(base), odsOperands(values) {}
   
     template <typename LateInst = PragmaArrayPartitionOp, typename = std::enable_if_t<std::is_same_v<LateInst, PragmaArrayPartitionOp>>>
     PragmaArrayPartitionOpGenericAdaptor(RangeT values, LateInst op) : Base(op), odsOperands(values) {}
   
     std::pair<unsigned, unsigned> getODSOperandIndexAndLength(unsigned index) {
       return Base::getODSOperandIndexAndLength(index, odsOperands.size());
     }
   
     RangeT getODSOperands(unsigned index) {
       auto valueRange = getODSOperandIndexAndLength(index);
       return {std::next(odsOperands.begin(), valueRange.first),
                std::next(odsOperands.begin(), valueRange.first + valueRange.second)};
     }
   
     ValueT getVariable() {
       return (*getODSOperands(0).begin());
     }
   
     RangeT getOperands() {
       return odsOperands;
     }
   
   private:
     RangeT odsOperands;
   };

   namespace detail {
   
   class PragmaDataflowOpGenericAdaptorBase {
   public:
     using Properties = ::mlir::EmptyProperties;
   protected:
     ::mlir::DictionaryAttr odsAttrs;
     ::std::optional<::mlir::OperationName> odsOpName;
     Properties properties;
     ::mlir::RegionRange odsRegions;
   public:
     PragmaDataflowOpGenericAdaptorBase(::mlir::DictionaryAttr attrs, const Properties &properties, ::mlir::RegionRange regions = {}) : odsAttrs(attrs), properties(properties), odsRegions(regions) {  if (odsAttrs)
         odsOpName.emplace("emithls.pragma.dataflow", odsAttrs.getContext());
     }
   
     PragmaDataflowOpGenericAdaptorBase(PragmaDataflowOp op);
   
     std::pair<unsigned, unsigned> getODSOperandIndexAndLength(unsigned index, unsigned odsOperandsSize) {
       return {index, 1};
     }
   
     const Properties &getProperties() {
       return properties;
     }
   
     ::mlir::DictionaryAttr getAttributes() {
       return odsAttrs;
     }
   
     ::mlir::Region &getBody() {
       return *odsRegions[0];
     }
   
     ::mlir::RegionRange getRegions() {
       return odsRegions;
     }
   
   };
   
   } // namespace detail
   template <typename RangeT>
   class PragmaDataflowOpGenericAdaptor : public detail::PragmaDataflowOpGenericAdaptorBase {
     using ValueT = ::llvm::detail::ValueOfRange<RangeT>;
     using Base = detail::PragmaDataflowOpGenericAdaptorBase;
   public:
     PragmaDataflowOpGenericAdaptor(RangeT values, ::mlir::DictionaryAttr attrs, const Properties &properties, ::mlir::RegionRange regions = {}) : Base(attrs, properties, regions), odsOperands(values) {}
   
     PragmaDataflowOpGenericAdaptor(RangeT values, ::mlir::DictionaryAttr attrs, ::mlir::PropertyRef properties, ::mlir::RegionRange regions = {}) : PragmaDataflowOpGenericAdaptor(values, attrs, (properties ? *properties.as<Properties *>() : Properties{}), regions) {}
   
     PragmaDataflowOpGenericAdaptor(RangeT values, ::mlir::DictionaryAttr attrs = nullptr) : PragmaDataflowOpGenericAdaptor(values, attrs, Properties{}, {}) {}
   
     PragmaDataflowOpGenericAdaptor(RangeT values, const PragmaDataflowOpGenericAdaptorBase &base) : Base(base), odsOperands(values) {}
   
     template <typename LateInst = PragmaDataflowOp, typename = std::enable_if_t<std::is_same_v<LateInst, PragmaDataflowOp>>>
     PragmaDataflowOpGenericAdaptor(RangeT values, LateInst op) : Base(op), odsOperands(values) {}
   
     std::pair<unsigned, unsigned> getODSOperandIndexAndLength(unsigned index) {
       return Base::getODSOperandIndexAndLength(index, odsOperands.size());
     }
   
     RangeT getODSOperands(unsigned index) {
       auto valueRange = getODSOperandIndexAndLength(index);
       return {std::next(odsOperands.begin(), valueRange.first),
                std::next(odsOperands.begin(), valueRange.first + valueRange.second)};
     }
   
     RangeT getOperands() {
       return odsOperands;
     }
   
   private:
     RangeT odsOperands;
   };
   class PragmaDataflowOpAdaptor : public PragmaDataflowOpGenericAdaptor<::mlir::ValueRange> {
   public:
     using PragmaDataflowOpGenericAdaptor::PragmaDataflowOpGenericAdaptor;
     PragmaDataflowOpAdaptor(PragmaDataflowOp op);
   
     ::llvm::LogicalResult verify(::mlir::Location loc);
   };
   class PragmaDataflowOp : public ::mlir::Op<PragmaDataflowOp, ::mlir::OpTrait::OneRegion, ::mlir::OpTrait::ZeroResults, ::mlir::OpTrait::ZeroSuccessors, ::mlir::OpTrait::ZeroOperands, ::mlir::OpTrait::NoTerminator, ::mlir::OpTrait::OpInvariants, ::mlir::emithls::PragmaInterface::Trait> {
   public:
     using Op::Op;
     using Op::print;
     using Adaptor = PragmaDataflowOpAdaptor;
     template <typename RangeT>
     using GenericAdaptor = PragmaDataflowOpGenericAdaptor<RangeT>;
     using FoldAdaptor = GenericAdaptor<::llvm::ArrayRef<::mlir::Attribute>>;
     using Properties = FoldAdaptor::Properties;
     static ::llvm::ArrayRef<::llvm::StringRef> getAttributeNames() {
       return {};
     }
   
     static constexpr ::llvm::StringLiteral getOperationName() {
       return ::llvm::StringLiteral("emithls.pragma.dataflow");
     }
   
     std::pair<unsigned, unsigned> getODSOperandIndexAndLength(unsigned index) {
       return {index, 1};
     }
   
     ::mlir::Operation::operand_range getODSOperands(unsigned index) {
       auto valueRange = getODSOperandIndexAndLength(index);
       return {std::next(getOperation()->operand_begin(), valueRange.first),
                std::next(getOperation()->operand_begin(), valueRange.first + valueRange.second)};
     }
   
     std::pair<unsigned, unsigned> getODSResultIndexAndLength(unsigned index) {
       return {index, 1};
     }
   
     ::mlir::Operation::result_range getODSResults(unsigned index) {
       auto valueRange = getODSResultIndexAndLength(index);
       return {std::next(getOperation()->result_begin(), valueRange.first),
                std::next(getOperation()->result_begin(), valueRange.first + valueRange.second)};
     }
   
     ::mlir::Region &getBody() {
       return (*this)->getRegion(0);
     }
   
     static ::llvm::LogicalResult setPropertiesFromAttr(Properties &prop, ::mlir::Attribute attr, ::llvm::function_ref<::mlir::InFlightDiagnostic()> emitError);
     static ::mlir::Attribute getPropertiesAsAttr(::mlir::MLIRContext *ctx, const Properties &prop);
     static llvm::hash_code computePropertiesHash(const Properties &prop);
     static std::optional<mlir::Attribute> getInherentAttr(::mlir::MLIRContext *ctx, const Properties &prop, llvm::StringRef name);
     static void setInherentAttr(Properties &prop, llvm::StringRef name, mlir::Attribute value);
     static void populateInherentAttrs(::mlir::MLIRContext *ctx, const Properties &prop, ::mlir::NamedAttrList &attrs);
     static ::llvm::LogicalResult verifyInherentAttrs(::mlir::OperationName opName, ::mlir::NamedAttrList &attrs, llvm::function_ref<::mlir::InFlightDiagnostic()> emitError);
     static void build(::mlir::OpBuilder &odsBuilder, ::mlir::OperationState &odsState, function_ref<void(OpBuilder &, Location)> bodyBuilder = nullptr);
     static PragmaDataflowOp create(::mlir::OpBuilder &builder, ::mlir::Location location, function_ref<void(OpBuilder &, Location)> bodyBuilder = nullptr);
     static PragmaDataflowOp create(::mlir::ImplicitLocOpBuilder &builder, function_ref<void(OpBuilder &, Location)> bodyBuilder = nullptr);
     ::llvm::LogicalResult verifyInvariantsImpl();
     ::llvm::LogicalResult verifyInvariants();
     static ::mlir::ParseResult parse(::mlir::OpAsmParser &parser, ::mlir::OperationState &result);
     void print(::mlir::OpAsmPrinter &_odsPrinter);
   public:
   };
   
   } // namespace mlir::emithls

   //===----------------------------------------------------------------------===//
   // ::mlir::emithls::PragmaInlineOp declarations

   namespace detail {
   
   class PragmaInlineOpGenericAdaptorBase {
   public:
     struct Properties {
       using offTy = ::mlir::UnitAttr;
       offTy off;
   
       auto getOff() const {
         auto &propStorage = this->off;
         return ::llvm::dyn_cast_or_null<::mlir::UnitAttr>(propStorage);
       }
       void setOff(const ::mlir::UnitAttr &propValue) {
         this->off = propValue;
       }
       bool operator==(const Properties &rhs) const {
         return 
           rhs.off == this->off &&
           true;
       }
       bool operator!=(const Properties &rhs) const {
         return !(*this == rhs);
       }
     };
   protected:
     ::mlir::DictionaryAttr odsAttrs;
     ::std::optional<::mlir::OperationName> odsOpName;
     Properties properties;
     ::mlir::RegionRange odsRegions;
   public:
     PragmaInlineOpGenericAdaptorBase(::mlir::DictionaryAttr attrs, const Properties &properties, ::mlir::RegionRange regions = {}) : odsAttrs(attrs), properties(properties), odsRegions(regions) {  if (odsAttrs)
         odsOpName.emplace("emithls.pragma.inline", odsAttrs.getContext());
     }
   
     PragmaInlineOpGenericAdaptorBase(PragmaInlineOp op);
   
     std::pair<unsigned, unsigned> getODSOperandIndexAndLength(unsigned index, unsigned odsOperandsSize) {
       return {index, 1};
     }
   
     const Properties &getProperties() {
       return properties;
     }
   
     ::mlir::DictionaryAttr getAttributes() {
       return odsAttrs;
     }
   
     ::mlir::UnitAttr getOffAttr() {
       auto attr = ::llvm::dyn_cast_or_null<::mlir::UnitAttr>(getProperties().off);
       return attr;
     }
   
     ::std::optional<bool> getOff();
   };
   
   } // namespace detail

   class PragmaInlineOp : public ::mlir::Op<PragmaInlineOp, ::mlir::OpTrait::ZeroRegions, ::mlir::OpTrait::ZeroResults, ::mlir::OpTrait::ZeroSuccessors, ::mlir::OpTrait::ZeroOperands, ::mlir::OpTrait::OpInvariants, ::mlir::BytecodeOpInterface::Trait, ::mlir::emithls::PragmaInterface::Trait> {
   public:
     using Op::Op;
     using Op::print;
     using Adaptor = PragmaInlineOpAdaptor;
     template <typename RangeT>
     using GenericAdaptor = PragmaInlineOpGenericAdaptor<RangeT>;
     using FoldAdaptor = GenericAdaptor<::llvm::ArrayRef<::mlir::Attribute>>;
     using Properties = FoldAdaptor::Properties;
     static ::llvm::ArrayRef<::llvm::StringRef> getAttributeNames() {
       static ::llvm::StringRef attrNames[] = {::llvm::StringRef("off")};
       return ::llvm::ArrayRef(attrNames);
     }
   
     ::mlir::StringAttr getOffAttrName() {
       return getAttributeNameForIndex(0);
     }
   
     static ::mlir::StringAttr getOffAttrName(::mlir::OperationName name) {
       return getAttributeNameForIndex(name, 0);
     }
   
     static constexpr ::llvm::StringLiteral getOperationName() {
       return ::llvm::StringLiteral("emithls.pragma.inline");
     }
   
     std::pair<unsigned, unsigned> getODSOperandIndexAndLength(unsigned index) {
       return {index, 1};
     }
   
     ::mlir::Operation::operand_range getODSOperands(unsigned index) {
       auto valueRange = getODSOperandIndexAndLength(index);
       return {std::next(getOperation()->operand_begin(), valueRange.first),
                std::next(getOperation()->operand_begin(), valueRange.first + valueRange.second)};
     }
   
     std::pair<unsigned, unsigned> getODSResultIndexAndLength(unsigned index) {
       return {index, 1};
     }
   
     ::mlir::Operation::result_range getODSResults(unsigned index) {
       auto valueRange = getODSResultIndexAndLength(index);
       return {std::next(getOperation()->result_begin(), valueRange.first),
                std::next(getOperation()->result_begin(), valueRange.first + valueRange.second)};
     }
   
     static ::llvm::LogicalResult setPropertiesFromAttr(Properties &prop, ::mlir::Attribute attr, ::llvm::function_ref<::mlir::InFlightDiagnostic()> emitError);
     static ::mlir::Attribute getPropertiesAsAttr(::mlir::MLIRContext *ctx, const Properties &prop);
     static llvm::hash_code computePropertiesHash(const Properties &prop);
     static std::optional<mlir::Attribute> getInherentAttr(::mlir::MLIRContext *ctx, const Properties &prop, llvm::StringRef name);
     static void setInherentAttr(Properties &prop, llvm::StringRef name, mlir::Attribute value);
     static void populateInherentAttrs(::mlir::MLIRContext *ctx, const Properties &prop, ::mlir::NamedAttrList &attrs);
     static ::llvm::LogicalResult verifyInherentAttrs(::mlir::OperationName opName, ::mlir::NamedAttrList &attrs, llvm::function_ref<::mlir::InFlightDiagnostic()> emitError);
     static ::llvm::LogicalResult readProperties(::mlir::DialectBytecodeReader &reader, ::mlir::OperationState &state);
     void writeProperties(::mlir::DialectBytecodeWriter &writer);
     ::mlir::UnitAttr getOffAttr() {
       return ::llvm::dyn_cast_or_null<::mlir::UnitAttr>(getProperties().off);
     }
   
     ::std::optional<bool> getOff();
     void setOffAttr(::mlir::UnitAttr attr) {
       getProperties().off = attr;
     }
   
     void setOff(bool attrValue);
     ::mlir::Attribute removeOffAttr() {
         auto attr = getProperties().off;
         getProperties().off = {};
         return attr;
     }
   
     static void build(::mlir::OpBuilder &odsBuilder, ::mlir::OperationState &odsState, bool off = false);
     static PragmaInlineOp create(::mlir::OpBuilder &builder, ::mlir::Location location, bool off = false);
     static PragmaInlineOp create(::mlir::ImplicitLocOpBuilder &builder, bool off = false);
     static ::mlir::ParseResult parse(::mlir::OpAsmParser &parser, ::mlir::OperationState &result);
     void print(::mlir::OpAsmPrinter &p);
     ::llvm::LogicalResult verifyInvariantsImpl();
     ::llvm::LogicalResult verifyInvariants();
     static void getCanonicalizationPatterns(::mlir::RewritePatternSet &results, ::mlir::MLIRContext *context);
     static ::llvm::LogicalResult setPropertiesFromParsedAttr(Properties &prop, ::mlir::Attribute attr, ::llvm::function_ref<::mlir::InFlightDiagnostic()> emitError);
   private:
     ::mlir::StringAttr getAttributeNameForIndex(unsigned index) {
       return getAttributeNameForIndex((*this)->getName(), index);
     }
   
     static ::mlir::StringAttr getAttributeNameForIndex(::mlir::OperationName name, unsigned index) {
       assert(index < 1 && "invalid attribute index");
       assert(name.getStringRef() == getOperationName() && "invalid operation name");
       assert(name.isRegistered() && "Operation isn't registered, missing a "
             "dependent dialect loading?");
       return name.getAttributeNames()[index];
     }
   
   public:
   };
   
   } // namespace mlir::emithls

   namespace detail {
   
   class PragmaPipelineOpGenericAdaptorBase {
   public:
     struct Properties {
       using intervalTy = ::mlir::IntegerAttr;
       intervalTy interval;
   
       auto getInterval() const {
         auto &propStorage = this->interval;
         return ::llvm::cast<::mlir::IntegerAttr>(propStorage);
       }
       void setInterval(const ::mlir::IntegerAttr &propValue) {
         this->interval = propValue;
       }
       using styleTy = ::mlir::emithls::PipelineStyleAttr;
       styleTy style;
   
       auto getStyle() const {
         auto &propStorage = this->style;
         return ::llvm::cast<::mlir::emithls::PipelineStyleAttr>(propStorage);
       }
       void setStyle(const ::mlir::emithls::PipelineStyleAttr &propValue) {
         this->style = propValue;
       }
       bool operator==(const Properties &rhs) const {
         return 
           rhs.interval == this->interval &&
           rhs.style == this->style &&
           true;
       }
       bool operator!=(const Properties &rhs) const {
         return !(*this == rhs);
       }
     };
   protected:
     ::mlir::DictionaryAttr odsAttrs;
     ::std::optional<::mlir::OperationName> odsOpName;
     Properties properties;
     ::mlir::RegionRange odsRegions;
   public:
     PragmaPipelineOpGenericAdaptorBase(::mlir::DictionaryAttr attrs, const Properties &properties, ::mlir::RegionRange regions = {}) : odsAttrs(attrs), properties(properties), odsRegions(regions) {  if (odsAttrs)
         odsOpName.emplace("emithls.pragma.pipeline", odsAttrs.getContext());
     }
   
     PragmaPipelineOpGenericAdaptorBase(PragmaPipelineOp op);
   
     std::pair<unsigned, unsigned> getODSOperandIndexAndLength(unsigned index, unsigned odsOperandsSize) {
       return {index, 1};
     }
   
     const Properties &getProperties() {
       return properties;
     }
   
     ::mlir::DictionaryAttr getAttributes() {
       return odsAttrs;
     }
   
     ::mlir::IntegerAttr getIntervalAttr() {
       auto attr = ::llvm::cast<::mlir::IntegerAttr>(getProperties().interval);
       return attr;
     }
   
     uint32_t getInterval();
     ::mlir::emithls::PipelineStyleAttr getStyleAttr() {
       auto attr = ::llvm::cast<::mlir::emithls::PipelineStyleAttr>(getProperties().style);
       return attr;
     }
   
     ::mlir::emithls::PipelineStyle getStyle();
   };
   
   } // namespace detail

     static ::mlir::ParseResult parse(::mlir::OpAsmParser &parser, ::mlir::OperationState &result);
     void print(::mlir::OpAsmPrinter &p);
     ::llvm::LogicalResult verifyInvariantsImpl();
     ::llvm::LogicalResult verifyInvariants();
     ::llvm::LogicalResult verify();
     static void getCanonicalizationPatterns(::mlir::RewritePatternSet &results, ::mlir::MLIRContext *context);
     static ::llvm::LogicalResult setPropertiesFromParsedAttr(Properties &prop, ::mlir::Attribute attr, ::llvm::function_ref<::mlir::InFlightDiagnostic()> emitError);
   private:
     ::mlir::StringAttr getAttributeNameForIndex(unsigned index) {
       return getAttributeNameForIndex((*this)->getName(), index);
     }
   
     static ::mlir::StringAttr getAttributeNameForIndex(::mlir::OperationName name, unsigned index) {
       assert(index < 2 && "invalid attribute index");
       assert(name.getStringRef() == getOperationName() && "invalid operation name");
       assert(name.isRegistered() && "Operation isn't registered, missing a "
             "dependent dialect loading?");
       return name.getAttributeNames()[index];
     }
   
   public:
   };

   //===----------------------------------------------------------------------===//
   // ::mlir::emithls::PragmaStreamOp declarations

   namespace detail {
   
   class PragmaStreamOpGenericAdaptorBase {
   public:
     struct Properties {
       using depthTy = ::mlir::IntegerAttr;
       depthTy depth;
   
       auto getDepth() const {
         auto &propStorage = this->depth;
         return ::llvm::cast<::mlir::IntegerAttr>(propStorage);
       }
       void setDepth(const ::mlir::IntegerAttr &propValue) {
         this->depth = propValue;
       }
       bool operator==(const Properties &rhs) const {
         return 
           rhs.depth == this->depth &&
           true;
       }
       bool operator!=(const Properties &rhs) const {
         return !(*this == rhs);
       }
     };
   protected:
     ::mlir::DictionaryAttr odsAttrs;
     ::std::optional<::mlir::OperationName> odsOpName;
     Properties properties;
     ::mlir::RegionRange odsRegions;
   public:
     PragmaStreamOpGenericAdaptorBase(::mlir::DictionaryAttr attrs, const Properties &properties, ::mlir::RegionRange regions = {}) : odsAttrs(attrs), properties(properties), odsRegions(regions) {  if (odsAttrs)
         odsOpName.emplace("emithls.pragma.stream", odsAttrs.getContext());
     }
   
     PragmaStreamOpGenericAdaptorBase(PragmaStreamOp op);
   
     std::pair<unsigned, unsigned> getODSOperandIndexAndLength(unsigned index, unsigned odsOperandsSize) {
       return {index, 1};
     }
   
     const Properties &getProperties() {
       return properties;
     }
   
     ::mlir::DictionaryAttr getAttributes() {
       return odsAttrs;
     }
   
     ::mlir::IntegerAttr getDepthAttr() {
       auto attr = ::llvm::cast<::mlir::IntegerAttr>(getProperties().depth);
       return attr;
     }
   
     uint32_t getDepth();
   };
   
   } // namespace detail
   template <typename RangeT>
   class PragmaStreamOpGenericAdaptor : public detail::PragmaStreamOpGenericAdaptorBase {
     using ValueT = ::llvm::detail::ValueOfRange<RangeT>;
     using Base = detail::PragmaStreamOpGenericAdaptorBase;
     static constexpr int odsIndex_variable = 0;
   public:
     PragmaStreamOpGenericAdaptor(RangeT values, ::mlir::DictionaryAttr attrs, const Properties &properties, ::mlir::RegionRange regions = {}) : Base(attrs, properties, regions), odsOperands(values) {}
   
     PragmaStreamOpGenericAdaptor(RangeT values, ::mlir::DictionaryAttr attrs, ::mlir::PropertyRef properties, ::mlir::RegionRange regions = {}) : PragmaStreamOpGenericAdaptor(values, attrs, (properties ? *properties.as<Properties *>() : Properties{}), regions) {}
   
     PragmaStreamOpGenericAdaptor(RangeT values, ::mlir::DictionaryAttr attrs = nullptr) : PragmaStreamOpGenericAdaptor(values, attrs, Properties{}, {}) {}
   
     PragmaStreamOpGenericAdaptor(RangeT values, const PragmaStreamOpGenericAdaptorBase &base) : Base(base), odsOperands(values) {}
   
     template <typename LateInst = PragmaStreamOp, typename = std::enable_if_t<std::is_same_v<LateInst, PragmaStreamOp>>>
     PragmaStreamOpGenericAdaptor(RangeT values, LateInst op) : Base(op), odsOperands(values) {}
   
     std::pair<unsigned, unsigned> getODSOperandIndexAndLength(unsigned index) {
       return Base::getODSOperandIndexAndLength(index, odsOperands.size());
     }
   
     RangeT getODSOperands(unsigned index) {
       auto valueRange = getODSOperandIndexAndLength(index);
       return {std::next(odsOperands.begin(), valueRange.first),
                std::next(odsOperands.begin(), valueRange.first + valueRange.second)};
     }
   
     ValueT getVariable() {
       return (*getODSOperands(0).begin());
     }
   
     RangeT getOperands() {
       return odsOperands;
     }
   
   private:
     RangeT odsOperands;
   };
   class PragmaStreamOpAdaptor : public PragmaStreamOpGenericAdaptor<::mlir::ValueRange> {
   public:
     using PragmaStreamOpGenericAdaptor::PragmaStreamOpGenericAdaptor;
     PragmaStreamOpAdaptor(PragmaStreamOp op);
   
     ::llvm::LogicalResult verify(::mlir::Location loc);
   };
   class PragmaStreamOp : public ::mlir::Op<PragmaStreamOp, ::mlir::OpTrait::ZeroRegions, ::mlir::OpTrait::ZeroResults, ::mlir::OpTrait::ZeroSuccessors, ::mlir::OpTrait::OneOperand, ::mlir::OpTrait::OpInvariants, ::mlir::BytecodeOpInterface::Trait, ::mlir::emithls::PragmaInterface::Trait> {
   public:
     using Op::Op;
     using Op::print;
     using Adaptor = PragmaStreamOpAdaptor;
     template <typename RangeT>
     using GenericAdaptor = PragmaStreamOpGenericAdaptor<RangeT>;
     using FoldAdaptor = GenericAdaptor<::llvm::ArrayRef<::mlir::Attribute>>;
     static constexpr int odsIndex_variable = 0;
     using Properties = FoldAdaptor::Properties;
     static ::llvm::ArrayRef<::llvm::StringRef> getAttributeNames() {
       static ::llvm::StringRef attrNames[] = {::llvm::StringRef("depth")};
       return ::llvm::ArrayRef(attrNames);
     }
   
     ::mlir::StringAttr getDepthAttrName() {
       return getAttributeNameForIndex(0);
     }
   
     static ::mlir::StringAttr getDepthAttrName(::mlir::OperationName name) {
       return getAttributeNameForIndex(name, 0);
     }
   
     static constexpr ::llvm::StringLiteral getOperationName() {
       return ::llvm::StringLiteral("emithls.pragma.stream");
     }
   
     std::pair<unsigned, unsigned> getODSOperandIndexAndLength(unsigned index) {
       return {index, 1};
     }
   
     ::mlir::Operation::operand_range getODSOperands(unsigned index) {
       auto valueRange = getODSOperandIndexAndLength(index);
       return {std::next(getOperation()->operand_begin(), valueRange.first),
                std::next(getOperation()->operand_begin(), valueRange.first + valueRange.second)};
     }
   
     ::mlir::TypedValue<::mlir::Type> getVariable() {
       return ::llvm::cast<::mlir::TypedValue<::mlir::Type>>(*getODSOperands(0).begin());
     }
   
     ::mlir::OpOperand &getVariableMutable() {
       auto range = getODSOperandIndexAndLength(0);
       return getOperation()->getOpOperand(range.first);
     }
   
     std::pair<unsigned, unsigned> getODSResultIndexAndLength(unsigned index) {
       return {index, 1};
     }
   
     ::mlir::Operation::result_range getODSResults(unsigned index) {
       auto valueRange = getODSResultIndexAndLength(index);
       return {std::next(getOperation()->result_begin(), valueRange.first),
                std::next(getOperation()->result_begin(), valueRange.first + valueRange.second)};
     }
   
     static ::llvm::LogicalResult setPropertiesFromAttr(Properties &prop, ::mlir::Attribute attr, ::llvm::function_ref<::mlir::InFlightDiagnostic()> emitError);
     static ::mlir::Attribute getPropertiesAsAttr(::mlir::MLIRContext *ctx, const Properties &prop);
     static llvm::hash_code computePropertiesHash(const Properties &prop);
     static std::optional<mlir::Attribute> getInherentAttr(::mlir::MLIRContext *ctx, const Properties &prop, llvm::StringRef name);
     static void setInherentAttr(Properties &prop, llvm::StringRef name, mlir::Attribute value);
     static void populateInherentAttrs(::mlir::MLIRContext *ctx, const Properties &prop, ::mlir::NamedAttrList &attrs);
     static ::llvm::LogicalResult verifyInherentAttrs(::mlir::OperationName opName, ::mlir::NamedAttrList &attrs, llvm::function_ref<::mlir::InFlightDiagnostic()> emitError);
     static ::llvm::LogicalResult readProperties(::mlir::DialectBytecodeReader &reader, ::mlir::OperationState &state);
     void writeProperties(::mlir::DialectBytecodeWriter &writer);
     ::mlir::IntegerAttr getDepthAttr() {
       return ::llvm::cast<::mlir::IntegerAttr>(getProperties().depth);
     }
   
     uint32_t getDepth();
     void setDepthAttr(::mlir::IntegerAttr attr) {
       getProperties().depth = attr;
     }
   
     void setDepth(uint32_t attrValue);
     static void build(::mlir::OpBuilder &odsBuilder, ::mlir::OperationState &odsState, Value variable, int32_t depth = 2);
     static PragmaStreamOp create(::mlir::OpBuilder &builder, ::mlir::Location location, Value variable, int32_t depth = 2);
     static PragmaStreamOp create(::mlir::ImplicitLocOpBuilder &builder, Value variable, int32_t depth = 2);
     static ::mlir::ParseResult parse(::mlir::OpAsmParser &parser, ::mlir::OperationState &result);
     void print(::mlir::OpAsmPrinter &p);
     ::llvm::LogicalResult verifyInvariantsImpl();
     ::llvm::LogicalResult verifyInvariants();
     ::llvm::LogicalResult verify();
     static ::llvm::LogicalResult setPropertiesFromParsedAttr(Properties &prop, ::mlir::Attribute attr, ::llvm::function_ref<::mlir::InFlightDiagnostic()> emitError);
   private:
     ::mlir::StringAttr getAttributeNameForIndex(unsigned index) {
       return getAttributeNameForIndex((*this)->getName(), index);
     }
   
     static ::mlir::StringAttr getAttributeNameForIndex(::mlir::OperationName name, unsigned index) {
       assert(index < 1 && "invalid attribute index");
       assert(name.getStringRef() == getOperationName() && "invalid operation name");
       assert(name.isRegistered() && "Operation isn't registered, missing a "
             "dependent dialect loading?");
       return name.getAttributeNames()[index];
     }
   
   public:
     Type getVariableType();
   };

   class PragmaUnrollOpGenericAdaptorBase {
   public:
     struct Properties {
       using factorTy = ::mlir::IntegerAttr;
       factorTy factor;
   
       auto getFactor() const {
         auto &propStorage = this->factor;
         return ::llvm::dyn_cast_or_null<::mlir::IntegerAttr>(propStorage);
       }
       void setFactor(const ::mlir::IntegerAttr &propValue) {
         this->factor = propValue;
       }
       bool operator==(const Properties &rhs) const {
         return 
           rhs.factor == this->factor &&
           true;
       }
       bool operator!=(const Properties &rhs) const {
         return !(*this == rhs);
       }
     };
   protected:
     ::mlir::DictionaryAttr odsAttrs;
     ::std::optional<::mlir::OperationName> odsOpName;
     Properties properties;
     ::mlir::RegionRange odsRegions;
   public:
     PragmaUnrollOpGenericAdaptorBase(::mlir::DictionaryAttr attrs, const Properties &properties, ::mlir::RegionRange regions = {}) : odsAttrs(attrs), properties(properties), odsRegions(regions) {  if (odsAttrs)
         odsOpName.emplace("emithls.pragma.unroll", odsAttrs.getContext());
     }
   
     PragmaUnrollOpGenericAdaptorBase(PragmaUnrollOp op);
   
     std::pair<unsigned, unsigned> getODSOperandIndexAndLength(unsigned index, unsigned odsOperandsSize) {
       return {index, 1};
     }
   
     const Properties &getProperties() {
       return properties;
     }
   
     ::mlir::DictionaryAttr getAttributes() {
       return odsAttrs;
     }
   
     ::mlir::IntegerAttr getFactorAttr() {
       auto attr = ::llvm::dyn_cast_or_null<::mlir::IntegerAttr>(getProperties().factor);
       return attr;
     }
   
     ::std::optional<uint32_t> getFactor();
   };

     static void setInherentAttr(Properties &prop, llvm::StringRef name, mlir::Attribute value);
     static void populateInherentAttrs(::mlir::MLIRContext *ctx, const Properties &prop, ::mlir::NamedAttrList &attrs);
     static ::llvm::LogicalResult verifyInherentAttrs(::mlir::OperationName opName, ::mlir::NamedAttrList &attrs, llvm::function_ref<::mlir::InFlightDiagnostic()> emitError);
     static ::llvm::LogicalResult readProperties(::mlir::DialectBytecodeReader &reader, ::mlir::OperationState &state);
     void writeProperties(::mlir::DialectBytecodeWriter &writer);
     ::mlir::IntegerAttr getFactorAttr() {
       return ::llvm::dyn_cast_or_null<::mlir::IntegerAttr>(getProperties().factor);
     }
   
     ::std::optional<uint32_t> getFactor();
     void setFactorAttr(::mlir::IntegerAttr attr) {
       getProperties().factor = attr;
     }
   
     void setFactor(::std::optional<uint32_t> attrValue);
     ::mlir::Attribute removeFactorAttr() {
         auto attr = getProperties().factor;
         getProperties().factor = {};
         return attr;
     }
   
     static void build(::mlir::OpBuilder &odsBuilder, ::mlir::OperationState &odsState, std::optional<int32_t> odsArg0);
     static PragmaUnrollOp create(::mlir::OpBuilder &builder, ::mlir::Location location, std::optional<int32_t> odsArg0);
     static PragmaUnrollOp create(::mlir::ImplicitLocOpBuilder &builder, std::optional<int32_t> odsArg0);
     static ::mlir::ParseResult parse(::mlir::OpAsmParser &parser, ::mlir::OperationState &result);
     void print(::mlir::OpAsmPrinter &p);
     ::llvm::LogicalResult verifyInvariantsImpl();
     ::llvm::LogicalResult verifyInvariants();
     static ::llvm::LogicalResult setPropertiesFromParsedAttr(Properties &prop, ::mlir::Attribute attr, ::llvm::function_ref<::mlir::InFlightDiagnostic()> emitError);
   private:
     ::mlir::StringAttr getAttributeNameForIndex(unsigned index) {
       return getAttributeNameForIndex((*this)->getName(), index);
     }
   
     static ::mlir::StringAttr getAttributeNameForIndex(::mlir::OperationName name, unsigned index) {
       assert(index < 1 && "invalid attribute index");
       assert(name.getStringRef() == getOperationName() && "invalid operation name");
       assert(name.isRegistered() && "Operation isn't registered, missing a "
             "dependent dialect loading?");
       return name.getAttributeNames()[index];
     }
   
   public:
   };
   
   } // namespace mlir::emithls
   MLIR_DECLARE_EXPLICIT_TYPE_ID(::mlir::emithls::PragmaUnrollOp)
   MLIR_DECLARE_EXPLICIT_TYPE_ID(::mlir::emithls::detail::PragmaUnrollOpGenericAdaptorBase::Properties)

         odsOpName.emplace("emithls.stream.read", odsAttrs.getContext());
     }

   template <typename RangeT>
   class StreamReadOpGenericAdaptor : public detail::StreamReadOpGenericAdaptorBase {
     using ValueT = ::llvm::detail::ValueOfRange<RangeT>;
     using Base = detail::StreamReadOpGenericAdaptorBase;
     static constexpr int odsIndex_stream = 0;
     static constexpr int odsIndex_indices = 1;
   public:
     StreamReadOpGenericAdaptor(RangeT values, ::mlir::DictionaryAttr attrs, const Properties &properties, ::mlir::RegionRange regions = {}) : Base(attrs, properties, regions), odsOperands(values) {}
   
     StreamReadOpGenericAdaptor(RangeT values, ::mlir::DictionaryAttr attrs, ::mlir::PropertyRef properties, ::mlir::RegionRange regions = {}) : StreamReadOpGenericAdaptor(values, attrs, (properties ? *properties.as<Properties *>() : Properties{}), regions) {}
   
     StreamReadOpGenericAdaptor(RangeT values, ::mlir::DictionaryAttr attrs = nullptr) : StreamReadOpGenericAdaptor(values, attrs, Properties{}, {}) {}
   
     StreamReadOpGenericAdaptor(RangeT values, const StreamReadOpGenericAdaptorBase &base) : Base(base), odsOperands(values) {}
   
     template <typename LateInst = StreamReadOp, typename = std::enable_if_t<std::is_same_v<LateInst, StreamReadOp>>>
     StreamReadOpGenericAdaptor(RangeT values, LateInst op) : Base(op), odsOperands(values) {}
   
     std::pair<unsigned, unsigned> getODSOperandIndexAndLength(unsigned index) {
       return Base::getODSOperandIndexAndLength(index, odsOperands.size());
     }
   
     RangeT getODSOperands(unsigned index) {
       auto valueRange = getODSOperandIndexAndLength(index);
       return {std::next(odsOperands.begin(), valueRange.first),
                std::next(odsOperands.begin(), valueRange.first + valueRange.second)};
     }
   
     ValueT getStream() {
       return (*getODSOperands(0).begin());
     }
   
     RangeT getIndices() {
       return getODSOperands(1);
     }
   
     RangeT getOperands() {
       return odsOperands;
     }
   
   private:
     RangeT odsOperands;
   };
   class StreamReadOpAdaptor : public StreamReadOpGenericAdaptor<::mlir::ValueRange> {
